\displaystyle~\quad\times\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{r}\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]\times\sin^{2}w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta),

\displaystyle~\mathcal{R}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\eta_{1}\cos w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)](\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})

\displaystyle~+2\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)[\phi_{2}^{(2)}-\phi_{2}^{(-2)}]

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)\sin 2\theta\psi_{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{r}\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{r}\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\Bigg[\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left[\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}-w_{\rho}(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})\right]\Bigg]\times\sin^{2}w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)\times\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta),

\displaystyle~\mathcal{R}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\cos w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)](\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})

\displaystyle~+2\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)[\phi_{1}^{(-2)}-\phi_{1}^{(2)}]

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\sin w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)]\sin 2\theta\psi_{2}

\displaystyle~-\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{r}\Bigg[\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left[\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}-w_{\rho}(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})\right]\Bigg]

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}^{2}}{r}\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)

\displaystyle~-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]\times\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta).

The derivation of above Lemma is rather lengthy, and we postpone it in Appendix
A.2
.

5.
Formulating the gluing system

We aim to find a real solution

u=U_{*}+\Phi,

so
S[u]=0
yields

{\partial}_{t}\Phi=\Delta\Phi-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}U_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Phi-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Phi\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}U_{*}-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Phi\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Phi+S[U_{*}].

We decompose
\Phi
into

\Phi(x,t)=\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}\Big[\Phi_{W}\left(y,t\right)+\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\left(y,t\right)\Big]+\Psi(x,t),\quad y=\frac{x-\xi(t)}{\lambda(t)},

where
\Phi_{W}
is in the
W
-direction,
\Phi_{W^{\perp}}
is on
W^{\perp}
,

\eta_{R}:=\eta\left(\frac{x-\xi(t)}{R(t)\lambda(t)}\right),

and
R(t)=\lambda^{-\beta}(t)
with
\beta>0
to be chosen later.

Then it is sufficient to find a desired solution
u
to (
1.9
) if the triple
(\Phi_{W},~\Phi_{W^{\perp}},~\Psi)
satisfies the gluing system:

(5.1)

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{W}=

\displaystyle\Delta_{y}\Phi_{W}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}W\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}\Phi_{W}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}\Phi_{W}\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}W

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\lambda^{2}\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}\quad\mbox{ in }~~\mathcal{D}_{2R},

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi_{W^{\perp}}=

\displaystyle\Delta_{y}\Phi_{W^{\perp}}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}W\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}\Phi_{W^{\perp}}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}W

\displaystyle+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\lambda^{2}\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\quad\mbox{ in }~~\mathcal{D}_{2R},

\displaystyle{\partial}_{t}\Psi=

\displaystyle\Delta_{x}\Psi+(1-\eta_{R})\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]+(1-\eta_{R})S[U_{*}]

\displaystyle+\Big[Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}\cdot\nabla_{x}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})-Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\partial_{t}\eta_{R}\Big]

\displaystyle-2(1-\eta_{R}){\partial}_{x_{1}}(U_{*}-U)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)

\displaystyle-2(1-\eta_{R}){\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(U_{*}-U)

\displaystyle+(1-\eta_{R})\Big[-\eta_{R}\dot{\gamma}J_{z}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\eta_{R}(\lambda^{-1}\dot{\xi}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}y)\cdot\nabla_{y}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\Big]

\displaystyle-2(1-\eta_{R}){\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\mbox{ in }~~{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T),

i.e.,
\Phi_{W}
and
\Phi_{W^{\perp}}
satisfy inner problem in the
W
-direction and on
W^{\perp}
, respectively, and
\Psi
solves the outer problem. Here

\mathcal{D}_{2R}:=\Big\{(y,t)\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times{\mathbb{R}}_{+}:~|y|\leq 2R(t),~t\in(0,T)\Big\},

\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}
,
E_{\eta_{1}}
are defined in (
6.2
), (
A.2
), and

(5.2)

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}=

\displaystyle\Pi_{W}\Bigg\{Q_{-\gamma}\bigg[-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}(U_{*}-U)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)

\displaystyle-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(U_{*}-U)

\displaystyle-\eta_{R}\dot{\gamma}J_{z}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\eta_{R}(\lambda^{-1}\dot{\xi}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}y)\cdot\nabla_{y}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})

\displaystyle-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\bigg]\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}=

\displaystyle\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Bigg\{Q_{-\gamma}\bigg[-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}(U_{*}-U)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)

\displaystyle-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(U_{*}-U)

\displaystyle-\eta_{R}\dot{\gamma}J_{z}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\eta_{R}(\lambda^{-1}\dot{\xi}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}y)\cdot\nabla_{y}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})

\displaystyle-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\bigg]\Bigg\}.

We will solve equations (
5.1
)
1
and (
5.1
)
2
with zero initial data and (
5.1
)
3
with non-trivial initial data

\Psi(x,0)=Z^{*}=(z_{1}^{*},z_{2}^{*},z_{3}^{*}),\quad x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}.

Problems (
5.1
)
1
and (
5.1
)
2
are the inner problem which captures the blow-up property, while (
5.1
)
3
describes the main character with singularities removed. We will solve these problems by developing corresponding linear theories and the fixed point argument in suitable weighted spaces. We notice that the inner problem (
5.1
)
2
is essentially a perturbation of the linearized harmonic map heat flow around the bubble
W
, and the inner problem (
5.1
)
1
is a perturbation of the parabolic linearized Liouville equation (cf. Lemma
2.1
and Remark
2.1.1
).

6.
Leading dynamics for the parameters

In this section, we capture the leading dynamics of the parameters
\lambda(t)
,
\xi(t)
,
\gamma(t)
,
c_{1}(t)
,
c_{2}(t)
, and these are in fact determined by orthogonality conditions in corresponding modes that are needed to ensure the existence of desired solutions with fast space-time decay (in the linear theories in Section
7.2
). We will choose
\lambda R\ll 1
, so
\eta_{1}(x)=\eta_{1}^{2}(x)=1
and
E_{\eta_{1}}=0
(cf. (
A.2
)) for
|x-\xi(t)|\leq 2\lambda R
. Our aim is to adjust the parameters such that the orthogonalities hold

(6.1)

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R}}\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\right)\cdot Z_{i,j}(y)dy=0,\quad i=0,1,~j=1,2,

\displaystyle\int_{B_{2R}}\left(\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}\right)\cdot\mathcal{Z}_{1,j}(y)dy=0,\quad j=1,2

for all
t\in(0,T)
, where the kernels
Z_{i,j}
and
\mathcal{Z}_{1,j}
are defined in (
2.2
). The reason for not requiring orthogonalities with the other three kernel functions
Z_{-1,1}
,
Z_{-1,2}
and
\mathcal{Z}_{0}
is to avoid further complications due to the introduction of new modulation parameters, and we shall use linear theories without orthogonality conditions in Section
7.2
together with regularity estimates to control these modes.

The goal of this section is to derive the leading dynamics governing these parameters by approximating (
6.1
). To do this, we decompose the remainder
\mathcal{R}_{*}
in
S[U_{*}]
as

(6.2)

\mathcal{R}_{*}:=\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}+\mathcal{R}_{*,1},

where
\mathcal{R}_{*,1}
is defined as

(6.3)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{*,1}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)\Bigg(\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+\sin w\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]\Bigg)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)\Bigg(\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+\sin^{3}w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]\Bigg)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta){\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos 2w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta){\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\sin w\cos w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta){\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{2}.

So the error reads

(6.4)

\displaystyle S[U_{*}]=

\displaystyle\Big[\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\mathcal{R}_{U}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)}+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1})+\eta_{1}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]\Big]+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}+\mathcal{R}_{*,1}+E_{\eta_{1}}.

In what follows,
S[U_{*}]
in the orthogonality conditions will be approximated by its main terms, and the analysis of the remainders will be done in Section
8.3
.

6.1.
\lambda
-
\gamma
system and translation parameter
\xi

We start from the RHS on
W^{\perp}
, and this turns out to yield the dynamics for
\lambda
and
\gamma
at mode
0
and for
\xi:=(\xi_{1},\ \xi_{2})
at mode
1
.

\bullet

Mode
0
on
W^{\perp}
:
\lambda
-
\gamma
system.

The orthogonality condition (
6.1
)
1
with
i=0
and
j=1,2
in the complex form implies

(6.5)

\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2R}\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}d\rho d\theta=0,

which is a complex system for
\lambda
and
\gamma
. We single out the main terms at mode
0
:

\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\Big((\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}]\Big)\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},0}.\\

We will deal with the remainder terms, turn out to be faster vanishing in time, when we solve the full problem via fixed point argument. From Lemma
2.3
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lambda^{-1}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}[\mathop{\rm div}(e^{-i\gamma}Z^{*}(q))+i{\rm{curl}}(e^{-i\gamma}Z^{*}(q))].

Also, from (
4.18
), (
4.19
), (
4.21
), and (
4.6
), we obtain

\displaystyle~\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\Big((\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}]\Big)\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\Big(\cos w{\rm Re}[(f_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)})e^{-i\gamma}]-\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\sin w\Big)+i{\rm Im}[(f_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)})e^{-i\gamma}]

\displaystyle~+2\lambda^{-1}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}{\rm Re}\Big[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\Big]+i2\lambda^{-1}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}{\rm Im}(e^{-i\gamma}\psi^{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\cos w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\frac{\lambda^{-1}\rho}{(1+\rho^{2})^{2}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\big(zk_{z}(z,t-s)-z^{2}k_{zz}(z,t-s)\big)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~-\cos w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\frac{\dot{\lambda}\rho}{1+\rho^{2}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}zk_{z}(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]-\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}

\displaystyle~+2\lambda^{-1}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\bigg(k(z,t-s)+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}(z,t-s)\bigg)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+i{\rm Im}\Bigg[\frac{\lambda^{-1}\rho}{(1+\rho^{2})^{2}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\big(zk_{z}(z,t-s)-z^{2}k_{zz}(z,t-s)\big)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~-i{\rm Im}\Bigg[\frac{\dot{\lambda}\rho}{1+\rho^{2}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}zk_{z}(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+i2\lambda^{-1}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}{\rm Im}\Big[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}k(z,t-s)ds\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\cos w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\lambda^{-1}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}}{t-s}\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+\cos w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\dot{\lambda}\rho w_{\rho}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}}{t-s}\zeta K_{\zeta}ds\Bigg]-\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}

\displaystyle~+2\lambda^{-1}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}}{t-s}\Big(K+\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+i{\rm Im}\Bigg[\lambda^{-1}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}}{t-s}\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+i{\rm Im}\Bigg[\dot{\lambda}\rho w_{\rho}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}}{t-s}\zeta K_{\zeta}ds\Bigg]+i2\lambda^{-1}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}{\rm Im}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}}{t-s}Kds\Bigg],

where

(6.6)

k(z,t)=\frac{1-e^{-\frac{z^{2}}{4t}}}{z^{2}},\quad\zeta=\frac{z^{2}}{t-s}=\frac{\lambda^{2}(1+\rho^{2})}{t-s},\quad K(\zeta)=\frac{1-e^{-\frac{\zeta}{4}}}{\zeta}.

Therefore, (
6.5
) at leading order gives the following system in complex form:

(6.7)

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{{\rm Re}}(p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)})}{t-s}\Gamma_{1}\left(\frac{\lambda(t)^{2}}{t-s}\right)ds+2\dot{\lambda}(t)+i\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{{\rm Im}}(p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)})}{t-s}\Gamma_{2}\left(\frac{\lambda(t)^{2}}{t-s}\right)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\Big[\mathop{\rm div}(e^{-i\gamma(t)}Z^{*}(q))+i{\rm{curl}}(e^{-i\gamma(t)}Z^{*}(q))\Big],

where

\displaystyle\Gamma_{1}(\tau)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\Bigg(\rho^{3}w_{\rho}^{3}\left[\Big(2K(\zeta)+4\zeta K_{\zeta}(\zeta)\frac{\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\Big)-\cos w\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}(\zeta)\right]+\cos w\lambda\dot{\lambda}\rho^{3}w^{2}_{\rho}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Bigg)_{\zeta=\tau(1+\rho^{2})}d\rho,

\displaystyle\Gamma_{2}(\tau)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\Big(\rho^{3}w_{\rho}^{3}\left[2K(\zeta)+\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}(\zeta)\right]+\lambda\dot{\lambda}\rho^{3}w^{2}_{\rho}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)_{\zeta=\tau(1+\rho^{2})}d\rho,

and we have used
\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{3}w_{\rho}^{3}d\rho=-2.
Clearly,

\Gamma_{1}(\tau)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &-1+O(\tau),~&~\tau\leq 1,\\
&O\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\right),~&~\tau>1,\\
\end{aligned}\quad\right.\quad\Gamma_{2}(\tau)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &-1+O(\tau),~&~\tau\leq 1,\\
&O\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\right),~&~\tau>1,\\
\end{aligned}\quad\right.

so it follows from (
6.7
) that

(6.8)

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}}\frac{p_{0}(s)}{t-s}ds=-2\Big[\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)+i{\rm{curl}}Z^{*}(q)\Big]+O(p_{0}(t))+o(1),

as
t\to T
. Recall
p_{0}(t):=-2(\dot{\lambda}+i\lambda\dot{\gamma})e^{i\gamma}
. We then proceed as
[
17
, Section 5, p. 372]
to obtain

\displaystyle\lambda(t)\sim\frac{T-t}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}},\quad\gamma(t)=\gamma_{*}=\arctan\frac{\mathop{\rm curl}Z^{*}(q)}{\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)}

as
t\to T
under the assumption that
\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)<0
. So the leading part of
\lambda(t)
is given by

\lambda_{*}(t)=\frac{(T-t)|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}},

where the
|\log T|
is a normalization factor. As we will see later, the dynamics of
\lambda
-
\gamma
system and
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system are in fact coupled, and this results in an extra restriction:

(6.9)

\cos\gamma_{*}\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)+\sin\gamma_{*}\mathop{\rm curl}Z^{*}(q)\leq 0,

roughly speaking, yielding more instability.

Remark 6.0.1
.

Both restrictions
\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)<0
and (
6.9
) can be achieved at the same time by choosing initial data
Z^{*}
such that

\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)<0,\quad\left|\frac{\mathop{\rm curl}Z^{*}(q)}{\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)}\right|\ll 1

since once
Z^{*}
is fixed the rotation angle
\gamma_{*}
is determined automatically by

\tan\gamma_{*}=\frac{\mathop{\rm curl}Z^{*}(q)}{\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)}.

In such case,
\gamma_{*}\ll 1
and thus one has (
6.9
).

\bullet

Mode
1
on
W^{\perp}
:
\xi
.

Similarly, the orthogonality condition (
6.1
)
1
with
i=1
and
j=1,2
in the complex form gives

(6.10)

\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2R}\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}w_{\rho}e^{-i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta=0.

From (
4.15
), (
4.16
), (
4.18
), (
4.19
), (
4.20
), (
4.21
), (
4.22
) and (
6.2
), the main terms that have contribution in
B_{2R}
in above integration are given by

\displaystyle~\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}]-\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*})\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}]-\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*})\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},1}+\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-2\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos we^{i\theta}\Big({\partial}_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)-i{\partial}_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\Big)+\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},1}

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}(\dot{\xi}_{1}-i\dot{\xi}_{2})e^{i\theta}+\rho w_{\rho}\sin w(\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}+i\dot{\gamma})\frac{e^{i\theta}}{2}(c_{1}-ic_{2})

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{2}e^{i(\theta-\gamma)}(\dot{\xi}_{1}-i\dot{\xi}_{2})\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds

\displaystyle~+\frac{w_{\rho}}{4}e^{i\theta}(\dot{\xi}_{1}-i\dot{\xi}_{2})\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}\Big(p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}+\bar{p}_{0}(s)e^{i\gamma(t)}\Big)\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+\frac{\lambda^{-1}}{4}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}e^{i\theta}\bar{p}_{1}(t)+\frac{\lambda^{-1}}{8}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{3}e^{i\theta}\int_{-T}^{t}\bar{p}_{1}(s)(zk_{z}-z^{2}k_{zz})(z,t-s)ds

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{4}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}e^{i\theta}\dot{\lambda}\int_{-T}^{t}\bar{p}_{1}(s)zk_{z}(z,t-s)ds-2\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos we^{i\theta}\int_{-T}^{t}\bar{p}_{1}(s)k(z,t-s)ds

\displaystyle~-\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w\frac{r^{2}}{z}e^{i\theta}\int_{-T}^{t}\bar{p}_{1}(s)k_{z}(z,t-s)ds

\displaystyle~+\frac{2}{r}(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)\Bigg(\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+\sin^{3}w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{r}\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta){\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]

\displaystyle~-i\frac{2}{r}\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos 2w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta){\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]

\displaystyle~+i\frac{2}{r}\sin w\cos w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta){\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right].

Then the dynamics given by (
6.10
) is approximately the following ODE:

(6.11)

\displaystyle\dot{\xi}_{1}-i\dot{\xi}_{2}\sim

\displaystyle CR^{-2}\Big({\partial}_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)-i{\partial}_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\Big)+O(|{\mathbf{c}}|)

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda}{4\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2R}\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\right)_{\mathbb{C},1}w_{\rho}e^{-i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta

for some constant
C
, where we have used the fact that
\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}\cos wd\rho=0
. For later purpose (when dealing with
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system), we will choose initial data such that

{\partial}_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)={\partial}_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)=0,

and

\lambda\Big|Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big|\lesssim\lambda^{\Theta},\quad|{\mathbf{c}}|\lesssim\lambda^{\Theta}(t)

for some
0<\Theta<1
. For the gluing procedure to work, we will eventually choose
\Theta
to be slightly bigger than
1/3
(cf. the final choice (
8.29
)). Because of this, also by estimates (
8.11
) and (
8.24
) that will be carried out later, one has

\lambda\left|\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\right|\lesssim\lambda^{3\Theta-1}.

Therefore, there exists a solution

\xi(t)=q+o((T-t)^{3\Theta})

for above ODE, where we recall
\lambda(t)\sim\frac{T-t}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}
.

6.2.
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system

Finally, we derive the asymptotic behavior of the translation parameters in the
W
-direction. From the linear theory of the inner problem in the
W
-direction, we need:

(6.12)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2R}\left[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\cdot W+\Big(Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}\Big)\cdot W\right]\sin w\cos\theta\rho d\rho d\theta=0,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2R}\left[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\cdot W+\Big(Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}\Big)\cdot W\right]\sin w\sin\theta\rho d\rho d\theta=0.

By (
4.15
), (
4.18
), (
4.20
), (
4.21
), (
4.22
) and (
6.2
), we have

\displaystyle~Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}]-\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*})\cdot W

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~Q_{-\gamma}\Big(\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\mathcal{R}_{U}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)}+(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1})+\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]\Big)\cdot W

\displaystyle~+Q_{-\gamma}(\mathcal{R}_{*}-\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*})\cdot W

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}\sin w(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}[\cos w(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta)+\dot{\lambda}\rho w_{\rho}](c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\sin w{\rm Re}[(f_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+f_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)})e^{-i\gamma}]+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\cos w

\displaystyle~-\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\left[\cos\theta(\dot{c}_{1}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}c_{1})+\sin\theta(\dot{c}_{2}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}c_{2})\right]

\displaystyle~+{\rm Re}\left[re^{-i\theta}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{z^{4}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{1}(s)(zk_{z}-z^{2}k_{zz})(z(r),t-s)ds+\mathcal{R}_{1}^{1}\right]\cos w

\displaystyle~+\frac{2}{\lambda}w_{\rho}\left({\rm Re}(e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0}))\cos w-\frac{1}{r}{\rm Re}(e^{-i\gamma}r\psi^{0})+\rho w_{\rho}{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}\psi_{z}^{1})]\right),

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{r}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)\Bigg(\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+\sin w\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]\Bigg),

and by Lemma
2.3
,

\displaystyle Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\cdot W\approx Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}^{2}\mathop{\rm div}(e^{-i\gamma}Z^{*}(q)))-\frac{2}{\lambda}w_{\rho}\sin w[\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\sin\theta]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\lambda}w_{\rho}\left[-\frac{1}{2}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}\mathop{\rm div}(e^{i\gamma}\bar{Z}^{*}(q))\cos(2\theta)+\frac{1}{2}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}{\rm{curl}}(e^{i\gamma}\bar{Z}^{*}(q))\sin(2\theta)\right].

So the terms in
\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\cdot W+Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}]-\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*})\cdot W\right)
that might contribute in (
6.12
) are given by

\displaystyle~-2\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w[\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\sin\theta]+Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W

\displaystyle~-\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}\sin w(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}[\cos w(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta)+\dot{\lambda}\rho w_{\rho}](c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)

\displaystyle~+\sin w{\rm Re}[(f_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)})e^{-i\gamma}]-\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\left[\cos\theta(\dot{c}_{1}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}c_{1})+\sin\theta(\dot{c}_{2}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}c_{2})\right]

\displaystyle~+{\rm Re}\left[re^{-i\theta}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{z^{4}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{1}(s)(zk_{z}-z^{2}k_{zz})(z(r),t-s)ds+\mathcal{R}_{1}^{1}\right]\cos w

\displaystyle~+\frac{2}{\lambda}w_{\rho}\left(\rho w_{\rho}{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}\psi_{z}^{1})]\right)

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{r}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{r}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)\sin w\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-2\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w[\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\sin\theta]+Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}^{2}\Big[\dot{\xi}_{1}c_{1}\cos^{2}\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}c_{2}\sin^{2}\theta+\dot{\xi}_{1}c_{2}\sin\theta\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}c_{1}\sin\theta\cos\theta\Big]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\Big[\sin 2\theta(\dot{\xi}_{1}c_{2}+\dot{\xi}_{2}c_{1})+\cos 2\theta(\dot{\xi}_{1}c_{1}-\dot{\xi}_{2}c_{2})\Big]-\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\left[\cos\theta\dot{c}_{1}+\sin\theta\dot{c}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle~+\sin w{\rm Re}[(\dot{\xi}e^{-i\theta})]{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+\cos w{\rm Re}\left[re^{-i\theta}\frac{\lambda^{2}}{z^{4}}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{1}(s)(zk_{z}-z^{2}k_{zz})(z(r),t-s)ds\right]

\displaystyle~+\cos w{\rm Re}\Bigg[e^{-i\theta}{\rm Re}[(\dot{\xi}e^{-i\theta})]\int_{-T}^{t}p_{1}(s)k(z(r),t-s)ds

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad\quad-\frac{re^{-i\theta}}{z^{2}}\left(\lambda\dot{\lambda}-{{\rm Re}}(re^{-i\theta}\dot{\xi}(t))\right)\int_{-T}^{t}p_{1}(s)zk_{z}(z(r),t-s)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+2\lambda^{-1}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\theta}\int_{-T}^{t}p_{1}(s)\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\right]

\displaystyle~+2\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta){\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]

\displaystyle~+2\lambda^{-1}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta){\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\Bigg].

So orthogonality condition (
6.12
)
1
implies

\displaystyle~-2\pi\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\lambda^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}wd\rho-\pi\dot{c}_{1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin wd\rho

\displaystyle~+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W\right)\sin w\cos\theta\rho d\rho d\theta

\displaystyle~+\pi\dot{\xi}_{1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho\sin^{2}w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]d\rho

\displaystyle~+\frac{\pi}{4}\lambda^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\sin w\cos w\int_{-T}^{t}{\rm Re}[p_{1}](s)(zk_{z}-z^{2}k_{zz})(z,t-s)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+\frac{\pi}{2}\dot{\lambda}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}\sin w\cos w\int_{-T}^{t}{\rm Re}[p_{1}](s)zk_{z}(z,t-s)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2\pi\lambda^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin w\int_{-T}^{t}{\rm Re}[p_{1}](s)\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2\pi\lambda^{-1}c_{1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]d\rho

\displaystyle~+2\pi\lambda^{-1}c_{1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\sin^{2}w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]d\rho

\displaystyle~=0,

and (
6.12
)
2
implies

\displaystyle~-2\pi\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\lambda^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}wd\rho-\pi\dot{c}_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin wd\rho

\displaystyle~+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W\right)\sin w\sin\theta\rho d\rho d\theta

\displaystyle~+\pi\dot{\xi}_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho\sin^{2}w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\Big(k(z,t-s)+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}(z,t-s)\Big)ds\Bigg]d\rho

\displaystyle~+\frac{\pi}{4}\lambda^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\sin w\cos w\int_{-T}^{t}{\rm Im}[p_{1}](s)(zk_{z}-z^{2}k_{zz})(z(r),t-s)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+\frac{\pi}{2}\dot{\lambda}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}\sin w\cos w\int_{-T}^{t}{\rm Im}[p_{1}](s)zk_{z}(z,t-s)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2\pi\lambda^{-1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin w\int_{-T}^{t}{\rm Im}[p_{1}](s)\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2\pi\lambda^{-1}c_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]d\rho

\displaystyle~+2\pi\lambda^{-1}c_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\sin^{2}w{\rm Re}\Bigg[\int_{-T}^{t}p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}\Big(k+\frac{r^{2}}{z}k_{z}\Big)(z,t-s)ds\Bigg]d\rho

\displaystyle~=0.

Neglecting the terms that have faster vanishing in time, we arrive at

\displaystyle~4\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)-2\lambda\dot{c}_{1}+\frac{\lambda}{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W\right)\sin w\cos\theta\rho d\rho d\theta

\displaystyle~-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\sin w\cos w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{1}](s)}{t-s}\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{1}](s)}{t-s}\Big(K(\zeta)+\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2c_{1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2c_{1}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Big(K(\zeta)+\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)dsd\rho

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~0,

and

\displaystyle~4\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)-2\lambda\dot{c}_{2}+\frac{\lambda}{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W\right)\sin w\sin\theta\rho d\rho d\theta

\displaystyle~-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\sin w\cos w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Im}[p_{1}](s)}{t-s}\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Im}[p_{1}](s)}{t-s}\Big(K(\zeta)+\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2c_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}dsd\rho

\displaystyle~+2c_{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Big(K(\zeta)+\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)dsd\rho

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~0,

where
\zeta
and
K(\zeta)
are defined in (
6.6
). Therefore, in complex form, the orthogonality condition in the
W
-direction reads

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds+2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}}+\Gamma_{4}[p_{0}]{\mathbf{c}}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle 4\big(\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)+i\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\big)+\frac{\lambda}{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W\right)\sin we^{i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta

where

\displaystyle p_{1}(t)=-2(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime},\quad{\mathbf{c}}(t)=c_{1}(t)+ic_{2}(t),

\displaystyle\Gamma_{3}(\tau)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho^{2}w_{\rho}^{2}\sin w\left[\cos w\zeta^{2}K_{\zeta\zeta}(\zeta)-2K(\zeta)-\frac{4\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}(\zeta)\right]_{\zeta=\tau(1+\rho^{2})}d\rho,

and

\displaystyle\Gamma_{4}[p_{0}]=

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}dsd\rho

\displaystyle-2\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w^{2}_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Big(K(\zeta)+\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}\Big)dsd\rho

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds

with

\displaystyle\Gamma_{5}(\tau)=-2\int_{0}^{+\infty}\rho w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\left[(1+w_{\rho})\frac{2\rho^{2}}{1+\rho^{2}}\zeta K_{\zeta}(\zeta)+w_{\rho}K(\zeta)\right]_{\zeta=\tau(1+\rho^{2})}d\rho.

It is direct to see that

\Gamma_{3}(\tau)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &-1+O(\tau),~&~\tau\leq 1,\\
&O\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\right),~&~\tau>1,\\
\end{aligned}\quad\right.\quad\Gamma_{5}(\tau)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &-\frac{2}{3}+O(\tau),~&~\tau\leq 1,\\
&O\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\right),~&~\tau>1,\\
\end{aligned}\quad\right.

and thus above reduced problem, at main order, can be written in the complex form as

\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\left(\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}ds\right){\mathbf{c}}=-4\big(\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)+i\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)\big)+f(t),

where

f(t):=-\frac{\lambda}{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z^{*}(q)]\cdot W\right)\sin we^{i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta.

In order for
p_{1}(t)
to vanish as
t\to T
, we require

\partial_{x_{1}}z^{*}_{3}(q)=\partial_{x_{2}}z^{*}_{3}(q)=0.

Then the new balancing condition becomes

(6.13)

\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\left(\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}ds\right){\mathbf{c}}=f(t).

Here,
f(t)
is essentially the contribution from the outer profile
\Psi
, and we will later see from the weighted space chosen for
\Psi
that

|f(t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t),\quad\lambda_{*}(t)=\frac{(T-t)|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}

for some
0<\Theta<1.

We now derive the leading asymptotic behavior of (
6.13
) by approximating it by an ODE, and the non-local parts remained will be solved by a linear theory in later section together with the fixed point argument.

Notice, by the leading order of
\lambda\sim\lambda_{*}
, that

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}ds=

\displaystyle~\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}ds+p_{1}(t)\log(t-s)\Big|_{s=t-\lambda^{2}(t)}^{t-(T-t)}+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)-p_{1}(t)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}ds+p_{1}(t)\left[\log(T-t)-2\log\lambda(t)\right]+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)-p_{1}(t)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{T-s}ds-p_{1}(t)\log(T-t)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{P}_{1}(t),

and exactly the same argument works for

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}ds\approx

\displaystyle~\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{T-s}ds-{\rm Re}[p_{0}(t)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]\log(T-t)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{P}_{0}(t).

We then have an approximation for equation (
6.13
):

\mathcal{P}_{1}(t)+\frac{2}{3}\mathcal{P}_{0}(t){\mathbf{c}}=f(t),

and we want to solve for
p_{1}(t)
from here.
Notice that

\displaystyle-\log(T-t)\mathcal{P}_{1}^{\prime}=\left[\log^{2}(T-t)p_{1}(t)\right]^{\prime},

\displaystyle-\log(T-t)\mathcal{P}_{0}^{\prime}=\left[\log^{2}(T-t){\rm Re}[p_{0}(t)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]\right]^{\prime}.

Recall that

p_{1}=-2(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime},\quad p_{0}=-2(\dot{\lambda}+i\lambda\dot{\gamma})e^{i\gamma}.

Then we get

(6.14)

2\Big[\log^{2}(T-t)(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}\Big]^{\prime}+\frac{4}{3}\Big[\log^{2}(T-t)\dot{\lambda}\Big]^{\prime}{\mathbf{c}}+\frac{2}{3}\log(T-t)\mathcal{P}_{0}{\mathbf{c}}^{\prime}=\log(T-t)f^{\prime}(t).

The third term above can in fact be approximated by

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}\approx

\displaystyle~2\dot{\lambda}-2{\rm Re}\Big[\mathop{\rm div}(e^{-i\gamma_{*}}Z^{*}(q))+i{\rm{curl}}(e^{-i\gamma_{*}}Z^{*}(q))\Big]

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle~-2{\rm Re}\Big[\mathop{\rm div}(e^{-i\gamma_{*}}Z^{*}(q))+i{\rm{curl}}(e^{-i\gamma_{*}}Z^{*}(q))\Big]

due to (
6.8
), where
\gamma_{*}
is the rotation angle. Let us now write

\mathbf{Z}:=-2{\rm Re}\Big[\mathop{\rm div}(e^{-i\gamma_{*}}Z^{*}(q))+i{\rm{curl}}(e^{-i\gamma_{*}}Z^{*}(q))\Big],

and thus equation (
6.14
) reads approximately

2\Big[\log^{2}(T-t)(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}\Big]^{\prime}+\frac{2}{3}\log(T-t)\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{\prime}=\log(T-t)f^{\prime}(t)

as
\frac{4}{3}\Big[\log^{2}(T-t)\dot{\lambda}\Big]^{\prime}{\mathbf{c}}
is relatively smaller in size. Above equation should be understood in the weak sense when solving rigorously
{\mathbf{c}}
. But for now, we only single out the main part, smooth for
t\in(0,T)
, of
{\mathbf{c}}
. The full solvability of
{\mathbf{c}}
is given in Section
9
.

Integrating above equation implies

-2\log^{2}(T-t)(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}+\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3}\int_{t}^{T}\log(T-s){\mathbf{c}}^{\prime}(s)ds=-f(t)\log(T-t)+\int_{t}^{T}\frac{f(s)}{T-s}ds

which is again approximately

(6.15)

-2\log^{2}(T-t)(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}-\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3}\log(T-t){\mathbf{c}}(t)=-f(t)\log(T-t)+\int_{t}^{T}\frac{f(s)}{T-s}ds.

The equation for
\lambda{\mathbf{c}}
is given by

\left[(T-t)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})\right]^{\prime}=(T-t)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\left(\frac{f(t)}{2\log(T-t)}-\frac{1}{2\log^{2}(T-t)}\int_{t}^{T}\frac{f(s)}{T-s}ds\right).

Thanks to (
6.9
), i.e.,
\mathbf{Z}\geq 0
, one can find a solution
{\mathbf{c}}
which vanishes as
t\to T
. Indeed,

\displaystyle|\lambda{\mathbf{c}}|=

\displaystyle~\left|(T-t)^{\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\int_{t}^{T}(T-\tau)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\frac{\log(T-\tau)}{|\log T|}}\left(\frac{f(\tau)}{2\log(T-\tau)}-\frac{1}{2\log^{2}(T-\tau)}\int_{\tau}^{T}\frac{f(s)}{T-s}ds\right)d\tau\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\frac{|f(t)|}{|\log(T-t)|}\left|(T-t)^{\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\int_{t}^{T}(T-\tau)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\frac{\log(T-\tau)}{|\log T|}}d\tau\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\begin{cases}\frac{3|\log T|(T-t)|f(t)|}{\mathbf{Z}|\log(T-t)|^{2}},&~\mathbf{Z}>0,\\
\frac{(T-t)|f(t)|}{|\log(T-t)|},&~\mathbf{Z}=0,\\
\end{cases}

and

|{\mathbf{c}}|\lesssim\begin{cases}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t),&~\mathbf{Z}>0,\\
\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)\frac{|\log(T-t)|}{|\log T|},&~\mathbf{Z}=0\\
\end{cases}

because of
|f(t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)
. Throughout the rest of this paper, we consider the most representative case when
\mathbf{Z}>0
, so we have

(6.16)

|{\mathbf{c}}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t).

Remark 6.0.2
.

The case with
\mathbf{Z}=0
is in fact more special as the leading dynamics of
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system in such case reduces to the one similar to that of
\lambda
-
\gamma
system. The coupling between
\lambda
-
\gamma
system and
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system only appears in the case
\mathbf{Z}>0
.

7.
Linear theories

In this section, we give the linear theories concerning the a priori estimates for the linear problem of outer problem (
5.1
)
3
, and the inner problems in the
W
-direction (
5.1
)
1
and on
W^{\perp}
(
5.1
)
2
.

7.1.
Linear theory for the outer problem

For
q\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
and
T>0
sufficiently small, we consider the problem

(7.1)

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\psi_{t}=\Delta_{x}\psi+g(x,t)&{\quad\mbox{in }}~{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T),\\
\psi(x,0)=Z^{*}(x)&{\quad\mbox{in }}~{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\end{cases}

for smooth initial value
Z^{*}
with compact support. The RHS
g
of (
7.1
) is assumed to be bounded with respect to some weights that appear in the outer problem (
5.1
)
3
.
Thus we define the weights

(7.2)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \varrho_{1}&:=\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\leq 3\lambda_{*}R\}},\\
\varrho_{2}&:=T^{-\sigma_{0}}\frac{\lambda_{*}^{1-\sigma_{0}}}{r^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq\lambda_{*}R\}},\\
\varrho_{3}&:=T^{-\sigma_{0}},\end{aligned}\right.

where
r=|x-q|
,
\Theta>0
and
\sigma_{0}>0
is small.
For a function
g(x,t)
we define the
L^{\infty}
-weighted norm

(7.3)

\displaystyle\|g\|_{**}:=\sup_{\gamma\times(0,T)}\Big(1+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\varrho_{i}(x,t)\,\Big)^{-1}{|g(x,t)|}.

We define the
L^{\infty}
-weighted norm for
\psi

\displaystyle\|\psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}

\displaystyle:=\lambda^{-\Theta}_{*}(0)\frac{1}{|\log T|\lambda_{*}(0)R(0)}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T))}+\lambda^{-\Theta}_{*}(0)\|\nabla_{x}\psi\|_{L^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T))}

\displaystyle~\quad+\sup_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T)}\lambda^{-\Theta-1}_{*}(t)R^{-1}(t)\frac{1}{|\log(T-t)|}|\psi(x,t)-\psi(x,T)|

\displaystyle~\quad+\sup_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T)}\,\lambda^{-\Theta}_{*}(t)|\nabla_{x}\psi(x,t)-\nabla_{x}\psi(x,T)|

(7.4)

\displaystyle~\quad+\sup\lambda^{-\Theta}_{*}(t)(\lambda_{*}(t)R(t))^{\alpha}\frac{|\nabla_{x}\psi(x,t)-\nabla_{x}\psi(x^{\prime},t^{\prime})|}{(|x-x^{\prime}|^{2}+|t-t^{\prime}|)^{\alpha/2}},

where
\Theta\in(0,1)
,
\alpha\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
, and the last supremum is taken in the region

x,\,x^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{2},\quad t,\,t^{\prime}\in(0,T),\quad|x-x^{\prime}|\leq 2\lambda_{*}(t)R(t),\quad|t-t^{\prime}|<\frac{1}{4}(T-t).

Also, we choose the initial data
Z^{*}(x)
so that

(7.5)

\psi_{1}(q,T)=\psi_{2}(q,T)=\psi_{3}(q,T)={\partial}_{x_{1}}\psi_{3}(q,T)={\partial}_{x_{2}}\psi_{3}(q,T)=0.

This can be achieved by the following: choose regular maps
\mathscr{Z}_{k}
with compact support,
k=1,\dots,5
satisfying

\mathscr{Z}_{k}(q)=e^{(5)}_{k}

where
\{e^{(5)}_{k}\}_{k=1}^{5}
forms an orthonormal basis of
{\mathbb{R}}^{5}
. We choose
\mathscr{C}_{k}
so that

(7.6)

\displaystyle\Big(\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\bullet~\mathcal{G},~[\nabla_{x}(\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\bullet~\mathcal{G})]_{3}\Big)(q,T)+\Big(\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ~Z^{*},~[\nabla_{x}(\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ Z^{*})]_{3}\Big)(q,T)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\mathscr{C}_{k}(\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ\mathscr{Z}_{k},~[\nabla_{x}(\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ\mathscr{Z}_{k})]_{3})(q,T)=0.

The first three and the last two vanishing conditions in (
7.5
) are needed in the gluing process and in
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system (i.e.
p_{1}(t)
), respectively.

We shall measure the solution
\psi
to the problem (
7.1
)
in the norm
\|\ \|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}
defined in (
7.4
). We invoke some useful estimates proved in
[
17
, Appendix A]
as follows.

Proposition 7.1

(
[
17
]
)
.

For
T>0
sufficiently small, there is a linear operator that maps a function
g:{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T)\to{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
with
\|g\|_{**}<\infty
into
\psi
which solves problem (
7.1
).
Moreover, the following estimate holds

\displaystyle\|\psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\leq C\|g\|_{**},

where
\alpha\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.

7.2.
Linear theories for the inner problems

For the inner problem, we consider the model equation for the inner problem as follows.

(7.7)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}\phi=L_{W}[\phi]+h(y,\tau),&~|y|\leq 2R(t(\tau)),~\tau\in(\tau_{0},+\infty),\\
&\phi(y,\tau_{0})=0,&~|y|\leq 2R(t(\tau_{0})),\\
\end{aligned}\right.

where

\tau=\tau(t)=\tau_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{ds}{\lambda(s)^{2}},

for
\tau_{0}
satisfying
t(\tau_{0})=0
, and
L_{W}
is the linearized operator defined in (
2.1
). We write the solution
\phi=\phi(\rho,\theta,\tau)
,
y=\rho e^{i\theta}
of (
7.7
) as

\phi(\rho,\theta,\tau)=\phi_{1}(\rho,\theta,\tau)E_{1}+\phi_{2}(\rho,\theta,\tau)E_{2}+\phi_{0}(\rho,\theta,\tau)W.

We will deal with
\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi]=\phi_{1}(\rho,\theta,\tau)E_{1}+\phi_{2}(\rho,\theta,\tau)E_{2}
and
\Pi_{W}[\phi]=\phi_{0}(\rho,\theta,\tau)W
separately, and consider

(7.8)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])=L_{W}[\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi]]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h](y,\tau),&~|y|\leq 2R(t(\tau)),~\tau\in(\tau_{0},+\infty),\\
&\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi](y,\tau_{0})=0,&~|y|\leq 2R(t(\tau_{0})),\\
\end{aligned}\right.

and

(7.9)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}(\Pi_{W}[\phi])=L_{W}[\Pi_{W}[\phi]]+\Pi_{W}[h](y,\tau),&~|y|\leq 2R(t(\tau)),~\tau\in(\tau_{0},+\infty),\\
&\Pi_{W}[\phi](y,\tau_{0})=0,&~|y|\leq 2R(t(\tau_{0})).\\
\end{aligned}\right.

We will measure the RHS by the norm:

(7.10)

\|h\|_{\nu,a}:=\sup_{|y|\leq 2R(t),~t\in(0,T)}\lambda_{*}^{-\nu}(t)\langle y\rangle^{a}|h(y,t)|

if we use
(y,t)
variables and by

\|h\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell}:=\sup_{|y|\leq 2R(t),~t\in(0,T)}v^{-1}(\tau)\langle y\rangle^{\ell}|h(y,\tau)|

if we use
(y,\tau)
variables. Here,
v(\tau)
is some Hölder continuous function decaying in
\tau
as
\tau\to\infty
.

7.2.1.
Linear theory for the inner problem on
W^{\perp}

If we use the complex notation
W^{\perp}

(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}=\phi_{1}+i\phi_{2},

then the equation on
W^{\perp}
reads as

{\partial}_{\tau}(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}=\Delta(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}-\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}+\frac{8}{(1+\rho^{2})^{2}}(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}+i\frac{2(\rho^{2}-1)}{\rho^{2}(\rho^{2}+1)}{\partial}_{\theta}(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}+(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h])_{\mathbb{C}}

with zero initial data.
We further expand
(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}
and
(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h])_{\mathbb{C}}
in Fourier modes

(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi])_{\mathbb{C}}=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta},\quad(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h])_{\mathbb{C}}=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}h_{k}^{W^{\perp}}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}.

Then the complex-valued scalar
\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}
at mode
k
satisfies

(7.11)

\displaystyle{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}-\frac{1+k^{2}}{\rho^{2}}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}+\frac{8}{(1+\rho^{2})^{2}}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}-\frac{2k(\rho^{2}-1)}{\rho^{2}(\rho^{2}+1)}\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}+h_{k}^{W^{\perp}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}^{W^{\perp}}_{k}[\phi_{k}^{W^{\perp}}]+h_{k}^{W^{\perp}}

which is precisely the linearization of harmonic map equation around degree
1
harmonic map dealt with in
[
17
, Section 7]
. So we have:

Proposition 7.2
.

(
[
17
, Proposition 7.1]
)

Assume that
a\in(2,3)
,
\nu>0
, and
\|\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h]\|_{\nu,a}<+\infty
.
Let us write

\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h]=h_{0}^{W^{\perp}}+h_{1}^{W^{\perp}}+h_{-1}^{W^{\perp}}+h_{\perp}^{W^{\perp}}~\mbox{ with }~h_{\perp}^{W^{\perp}}=\sum_{k\not=0,\pm 1}h_{k}^{W^{\perp}}.

Then there exists a solution
\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi](h)
of problem (
7.8
), which defines
a linear operator of
\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h]
, and satisfies the following estimate for

|y|\leq 2R(t)
with
t\in(0,T)
:

\displaystyle\quad|\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi](y,t)|+(1+|y|)\left|\nabla_{y}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[\phi](y,t)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\,\min\left\{\frac{R^{5-a}(t)}{1+|y|^{3}},~\frac{R^{\frac{5-a}{2}}(t)}{1+|y|}\right\}\,\|h_{0}^{W^{\perp}}-\bar{h}_{0}^{W^{\perp}}\|_{\nu,a}+\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)R^{2}(t)}{1+|y|}\|\bar{h}_{0}^{W^{\perp}}\|_{\nu,a}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\,\left\|h_{1}^{W^{\perp}}-\bar{h}_{1}^{W^{\perp}}\right\|_{\nu,a}+\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)R^{4}(t)}{1+|y|^{2}}\left\|\bar{h}_{1}^{W^{\perp}}\right\|_{\nu,a}

\displaystyle\quad+\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\,\min\left\{\frac{R^{4-a}(t)}{1+|y|^{2}},~\log R\right\}\,\|h_{-1}^{W^{\perp}}-\bar{h}_{-1}^{W^{\perp}}\|_{\nu,a}+\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\log R(t)\,\|\bar{h}_{-1}^{W^{\perp}}\|_{\nu,a}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\,\|h_{\perp}^{W^{\perp}}\|_{\nu,a}.

Here

(7.12)

\bar{h}^{W^{\perp}}_{k}(y,t):=\sum_{j=1}^{2}\frac{\chi Z_{k,j}(y)}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\chi|Z_{k,j}|^{2}}\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}[h](z,t)\cdot Z_{k,j}(z)\,dz,\quad~k=0,\pm 1,~j=1,2,

with
Z_{k,j}
defined in (
2.2
), where

(7.13)

\chi(y,\tau)=\begin{cases}w_{\rho}^{2}(|y|)&\hbox{ if }|y|<2R(t),\\
0&\hbox{ if }|y|\geq 2R(t).\end{cases}

Via a re-gluing process, better estimates can be gained at mode
0
with a slight modification on the orthogonality condition. Consider the linear problem at mode
0
on
W^{\perp}
:

(7.14)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &~\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi=L_{W}\phi+h(\rho,t)+\sum_{j=1,2}\tilde{c}_{0j}Z_{0,j}w_{\rho}^{2},\qquad|y|\leq 2R(t),~t\in(0,T),\\
&~\phi\perp W,\quad\phi=0~\mbox{ on }~{\partial}B_{2R}\times(0,T),\quad\phi(\cdot,0)=0~\mbox{ in }~B_{2R(0)}.\end{aligned}\right.

Proposition 7.3
.

(
[
17
, Proposition 7.2]
)

Let
\sigma_{*}\in(0,1)
. Under the assumptions of Proposition
7.2
, there exists
(\phi,~\tilde{c}_{0j})
, linear in
h
, solving (
7.14
) such that

|\phi|+(1+\rho)|{\partial}_{\rho}\phi|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu}(t)\|h\|_{\nu,a}\min\left\{\frac{R^{\sigma_{*}(5-a)}(t)}{1+|y|^{3}},\frac{1}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\right\}

and such that

\tilde{c}_{0j}=\tilde{c}_{0j}[h]=-\frac{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}h\cdot Z_{0,j}}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}Z_{0,j}^{2}}-G[h],\quad j=1,2

for some operator
G
linear in
h
with

|G[h]|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu}R^{-\sigma_{*}\sigma^{\prime}}\|h\|_{\nu,a},\quad\sigma^{\prime}\in(0,~a-2).

More refined versions of above linear theory on
W^{\perp}
are obtained in
[
55
, Section 9]
(in a special case of
a=1
,
b=0
).

7.2.2.
Linear theory for inner problem in the
W
-direction

For the equation (
7.9
) in the
W
-direction, the use of

\Pi_{W}[\phi]=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\phi_{k}^{W}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta},\quad\Pi_{W}[h]=\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}h_{k}^{W}(\rho,\tau)e^{ik\theta}

gives equation in mode
k
:

(7.15)

\begin{cases}{\partial}_{\tau}\phi_{k}^{W}(\rho,\tau)=\mathcal{L}_{k}^{W}[\phi_{k}^{W}]+h^{W}_{k}(\rho,\tau),\quad(\rho,\tau)\in(0,\infty)\times(\tau_{0},\infty),\\
\phi^{W}_{k}(\rho,\tau_{0})=0,\quad\rho\in(0,\infty),\end{cases}

where

\mathcal{L}_{k}^{W}:={\partial}_{\rho\rho}+\frac{1}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}-\frac{k^{2}}{\rho^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+\rho^{2})^{2}}.

\bullet

Mode
0
.

In mode
k=0
, we establish a linear theory via the distorted Fourier transform (DFT).

Proposition 7.4
.

In (
7.15
) with
k=0
, if
\|h_{0}^{W}\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell}<\infty
with
\ell>\frac{3}{2}
, then there exists a solution
\phi_{0}^{W}
that satisfies the following estimate

\displaystyle|\phi^{W}_{0}(\rho,\tau)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|h_{0}^{W}\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)(\log\tau)^{2}+\tau^{-1}\log\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\log\tau+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\|h_{0}^{W}\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho>\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\}}\rho^{-1/2}\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

If, in addition,
2<\ell<\frac{5}{2}
and the orthogonality condition

(7.16)

\int_{0}^{\infty}h_{0}^{W}(\rho,\tau)\frac{\rho^{2}-1}{\rho^{2}+1}\rho d\rho=0\mbox{ \ for all \ }\tau>\tau_{0}

is satisfied, then the following estimate holds

|\phi^{W}_{0}(\rho,\tau)|\lesssim\|h_{0}^{W}\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell}\begin{cases}v(\tau)\langle\rho\rangle^{2-\ell}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho\leq\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\rho^{-1/2}\left(v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\right)&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho>\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\end{cases}.

We postpone the proof of Proposition
7.4
in Appendix
B.2
.

\bullet

Modes
k=\pm 1
.

We notice that
\mathcal{L}^{W}_{\pm 1}\equiv\mathcal{L}^{W^{\perp}}_{0}
, where
\mathcal{L}^{W^{\perp}}_{k}
is defined in (
7.11
) (on
W^{\perp}
), meaning that the linear theory for modes
\pm 1
in the
W
-direction is exactly the same as the one for mode
0
on
W^{\perp}
. So for modes
|k|=1
in the
W
-direction, we have

Proposition 7.5
.

(
[
17
, Lemma 7.1, Lemma 7.3]
)

For modes
k=\pm 1
in (
7.15
), there exists a solution
\phi_{k}^{W}
that satisfies the following estimate

\displaystyle|\phi_{k}^{W}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\,\min\left\{\frac{R^{5-a}(t)}{1+|y|^{3}},~\frac{R^{\frac{5-a}{2}}(t)}{1+|y|}\right\}\|h^{W}_{k}-\bar{h}^{W}_{k}\|_{\nu,a}+\frac{\lambda_{*}^{\nu}(t)R^{2}(t)}{1+|y|}\|\bar{h}_{k}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}.

Here
a\in(2,3)
,
\nu>0
, and

\displaystyle\bar{h}_{1}^{W}:=

\displaystyle~\frac{\chi\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(y)}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\chi|\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}|^{2}}\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\Pi_{W}[h](z,t)\cdot\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}(z)\,dz,

\displaystyle\bar{h}_{-1}^{W}:=

\displaystyle~\frac{\chi\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}(y)}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\chi|\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}|^{2}}\,\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\Pi_{W}[h](z,t)\cdot\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}(z)\,dz,

with
\chi
,
\mathcal{Z}_{1,k}
defined respectively in (
7.13
) and (
2.2
).

Remark 7.5.1
.

For the non-orthogonal parts in Proposition
7.2
and Proposition
7.5
, one can in fact relax the assumption on the spatial decay to
0<a<3
with estimates modified accordingly; see
[
17
, p. 394, Lemma 7.1]
.
Later we will use this version.

An analogue of Proposition
7.3
also holds true for modes
k=\pm 1
. Consider the linear problem in modes
\pm 1
in the
W
-direction:

(7.17)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &~\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\phi=L_{W}\phi+h(\rho,t)+\sum_{j=1,2}\hat{c}_{1j}\mathcal{Z}_{1,j}w_{\rho}^{2},\qquad|y|\leq 2R(t),~t\in(0,T),\\
&~\phi\perp W^{\perp},\quad\phi=0~\mbox{ on }~{\partial}B_{2R}\times(0,T),\quad\phi(\cdot,0)=0~\mbox{ in }~B_{2R(0)}.\end{aligned}\right.

We have the following refined estimates:

Proposition 7.6
.

Let
\sigma_{*}\in(0,1)
. Under the assumptions of Proposition
7.5
, there exists
(\phi,~\hat{c}_{1j})
, linear in
h
, solving (
7.17
) such that

|\phi|+(1+\rho)|{\partial}_{\rho}\phi|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu}(t)\|h\|_{\nu,a}\min\left\{\frac{R^{\sigma_{*}(5-a)}(t)}{1+|y|^{3}},\frac{1}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\right\}

and such that

\hat{c}_{1j}=\hat{c}_{1j}[h]=-\frac{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}h\cdot\mathcal{Z}_{1,j}}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}\mathcal{Z}_{1,j}^{2}}-\hat{G}[h],\quad j=1,2

for some operator
\hat{G}
linear in
h
with

|\hat{G}[h]|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\nu}R^{-\sigma_{*}\sigma^{\prime}}\|h\|_{\nu,a},\quad\sigma^{\prime}\in(0,~a-2).

\bullet

Higher modes
|k|\geq 2
.

Proposition 7.7
.

For modes
|k|\geq 2
in (
7.15
), there exists a solution
\phi^{W}_{k}
that satisfies

|\phi_{k}^{W}|\lesssim\frac{1}{k^{2}}\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\langle\rho\rangle^{2-a}\|h_{k}^{W}\|_{\nu,a},

where
a\in(2,3)
,
\nu>0
.

Proof.

We start with
k=2
(the same for
k=-2
). In such case,
\mathcal{L}^{W}_{2}
has a (non sign-changing) kernel function, denoted by
Z_{2}
, whose asymptotic behavior is
\rho^{2}
both near origin and at infinity. More generally, for
|k|\geq 2
,
\mathcal{L}^{W}_{k}
has kernel functions

\frac{\rho^{-k}(k\rho^{2}+k+\rho^{2}-1)}{\rho^{2}+1},\quad\frac{\rho^{k}(k\rho^{2}+k-\rho^{2}+1)}{\rho^{2}+1}.

We choose barrier function

\bar{\varphi}_{2}=2\|h_{2}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}\lambda_{*}^{\nu}\varphi_{2}

with
\varphi_{2}
solving

\mathcal{L}^{W}_{2}[\varphi_{2}]+\langle\rho\rangle^{-a}=0.

With Dirichlet boundary
\varphi_{2}(2R)=0
, above equation admits a unique solution given by

\varphi_{2}(\rho)=Z_{2}(\rho)\int_{\rho}^{2R}\frac{dr}{rZ^{2}_{2}(r)}\int_{0}^{r}\langle s\rangle^{-a}Z_{2}(s)sds

where
a\in(2,3)
. Then one has

|\varphi_{2}|\leq C\langle\rho\rangle^{2-a},

and thus

\displaystyle-\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\bar{\varphi}_{2}+\mathcal{L}^{W}_{2}[\bar{\varphi}_{2}]+h^{W}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle~2\nu\|h_{2}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\lambda_{*}^{\nu+1}|\varphi_{2}|-\|h_{2}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}\lambda_{*}^{\nu}\langle\rho\rangle^{-a}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\|h_{2}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}\lambda_{*}^{\nu}\langle\rho\rangle^{-a}\Big(2\nu C|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\lambda_{*}\langle\rho\rangle^{2}-1\Big)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle~0

since
|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\lambda_{*}R^{2}\ll 1
(we choose
\beta<\frac{1}{2}
in
R=\lambda_{*}^{-\beta}
). Therefore, the pointwise estimate for mode
k=2
follows. For the higher modes
|k|\geq 3
, similar argument works. Indeed, We choose barrier function

\bar{\varphi}_{2}=2\|h^{W}_{k}\|_{\nu,a}\lambda_{*}^{\nu}\varphi_{k}

with
\varphi_{k}
solving

\mathcal{L}^{W}_{k}[\varphi_{k}]+\langle\rho\rangle^{-a}=0,

where in this case,

|\varphi_{k}|\leq\frac{C}{k^{2}}\langle\rho\rangle^{2-a}

by variation of parameters. The proof is thus completed.
∎

We then combine Proposition
7.4
(returning to
(y,t)
variables), Proposition
7.5
and Proposition
7.7
and use a scaling argument together with parabolic regularity estimates to get gradient estimate for (
7.9
). We thus conclude the following linear theory for the inner problem in the
W
-direction.

Proposition 7.8
.

Assume that
a\in(2,\frac{5}{2})
,
\nu>0
, and
\|\Pi_{W}[h]\|_{\nu,a}<+\infty
.
Then there exists a solution
\Pi_{W}[\phi](h)
of problem (
7.9
), which defines
a linear operator of
\Pi_{W}[h]
, and satisfies the following estimate for

|y|\leq 2R(t)
with
t\in(0,T)
:

\displaystyle\quad|\Pi_{W}[\phi](y,t)|+(1+|y|)\left|\nabla_{y}\Pi_{W}[\phi](y,t)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\,\|h_{0}^{W}-\bar{h}_{0}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}+\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\log R(t)\,\|\bar{h}_{0}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k=\pm 1}\left(\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\,\min\left\{\frac{R^{5-a}(t)}{1+|y|^{3}},~\frac{R^{\frac{5-a}{2}}(t)}{1+|y|}\right\}\,\|h_{k}^{W}-\bar{h}_{k}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}+\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)R^{2}(t)}{1+|y|}\|\bar{h}_{k}^{W}\|_{\nu,a}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\,\Big\|h-h_{0}^{W}-h_{1}^{W}-h_{-1}^{W}\Big\|_{\nu,a}.

Remark 7.8.1
.

In practice, we are going to impose orthogonality conditions on the inner problems for modes
0
,
1
on
W^{\perp}
and modes
\pm 1
in the
W
-direction, and these orthogonalities yield the dynamics of the
\lambda
-
\gamma
system, translation parameter
\xi=(\xi_{1},\xi_{2})
and
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system, respectively. Under these orthogonalities, we will solve both inner solutions
\Phi_{W}
and
\Phi_{W^{\perp}}
to (
5.1
)
1
and (
5.1
)
2
in the wighted space:

(7.18)

\|\phi\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}:=\sup_{|y|\leq 2R(t),~t\in(0,T)}\left[\lambda^{\nu}_{*}(t)\,\max\left\{\frac{R^{\sigma_{*}(5-a)}(t)}{1+|y|^{3}},\frac{1}{1+|y|^{a-2}}\right\}\right]^{-1}\Big(|\phi(y,t)|+\langle y\rangle|\nabla_{y}\phi(y,t)|\Big)

for some
\sigma_{*},~\nu\in(0,1)
,
a\in(2,3)
.

8.
Solving the gluing system

In this section, we solve the full gluing system. We are going to work in the following weighted topologies.

•

RHS for both inner problems (
5.1
)
1
and (
5.1
)
2
:
\|\cdot\|_{\nu,a}
-norm defined in (
7.10
).

•

RHS for the outer problem (
5.1
)
3
:
\|\cdot\|_{**}
-norm defined in (
7.3
).

•

Inner solutions
\Phi_{W}
and
\Phi_{W^{\perp}}
:
\|\cdot\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}
-norm defined in (
7.18
).

•

Outer solution
\Psi
:
\|\cdot\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}
-norm defined in (
7.4
).

The constants measuring above weighted norms will be put into a specific range ensuring the implementation of the gluing process. We now start to estimate RHS in the gluing system (
5.1
) and reveal these restrictions on those constants. We are also going to use the leading asymptotics of the modulation parameters:

|\lambda|\sim\lambda_{*}=\frac{(T-t)|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}},\quad|\dot{\xi}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-1},\quad|c_{1}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta},\quad|c_{2}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta},

and recall

R=R(t)=\lambda_{*}^{-\beta}.

Our first assumption on the constants is the following:

(8.1)

0<\nu<1,\quad 2<a<3,\quad 0<\beta<1/2,\quad 0<\Theta<1/2.

8.1.
Non-local systems

As discussed in Section
6
, translation parameter
\xi
obeys essentially an ODE of first order, while the dynamics of
\lambda
-
\gamma
system and
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system are governed by integro-differential equations. The leading non-local operator might be regarded as the Abel’s integral operator in the end-point case, so the solvability is rather involved.

For the
\lambda
-
\gamma
system, we recall (
4.5
). To introduce the space for the parameter function
p_{0}(t)=-2(\dot{\lambda}+i\lambda\dot{\gamma})e^{i\gamma}
,
we recall the non-local operator
\mathcal{B}_{0}
appears at mode 0 on
W^{\perp}
is of the approximate form

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{0}[p_{0}]=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}}\frac{p_{0}(s)}{t-s}ds\,+O(p_{0}(t)).

For
\Theta\in(0,1)
,
\varpi\in{\mathbb{R}}
and a continuous function
g:[-T,T]\to\mathbb{C}
, we define the norm

(8.2)

\displaystyle\|g\|_{\Theta,\varpi}=\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\,(T-t)^{-\Theta}|\log(T-t)|^{\varpi}|g(t)|,

and for
\alpha\in(0,1)
,
m,~\varpi\in{\mathbb{R}}
, we define the semi-norm

[g]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi}=\sup\,(T-t)^{-m}|\log(T-t)|^{\varpi}\frac{|g(t)-g(s)|}{(t-s)^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}},

where the supremum is taken over
s\leq t
in
[-T,T]
such that
t-s\leq\frac{1}{4}(T-t)
.

The following proposition gives an approximate inverse of the non-local operator
\mathcal{B}_{0}
with a small remainder
\mathcal{R}_{0}
.

Proposition 8.1
.

(
[
17
, Proposition 6.5, Proposition 6.6]
)

Let
\alpha_{0},\frac{\alpha}{2}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
,
\varpi\in{\mathbb{R}}
. There is
\flat>0
such that if
\Theta\in(0,\flat)
and
m\leq\Theta-\frac{\alpha}{2}
, then for
h(t):[0,T]\to\mathbb{C}
satisfying

(8.3)

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &|h(T)|>0,\\
&T^{\Theta}|\log T|^{1+\sigma-\varpi}\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}+[h]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi-1}\leq C_{1},\end{aligned}\right.

for some
C_{1}>0
,
\sigma\in(0,1)
,
then, for
T>0
sufficiently small there exist two operators
\mathcal{P}
and
\mathcal{R}_{0}
so that
p_{0}=\mathcal{P}[h]:[-T,T]\to\mathbb{C}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{0}[p_{0}](t)=h(t)+\mathcal{R}_{0}[h](t),\quad t\in[0,T]

with

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{0}[h](t)|\leq C\Bigl(T^{\sigma}+T^{\Theta}\frac{\log|\log T|}{|\log T|}\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}+[h]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi-1}\Bigr)\frac{(T-t)^{m+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}}}{|\log(T-t)|^{\varpi}}.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}[h]=p_{0,\kappa}+\mathcal{P}_{1}[h]+\mathcal{P}_{2}[h],

with

\displaystyle p_{0,\kappa}(t)=\frac{\kappa|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}},\quad t\leq T,

where
\kappa=\kappa[h]
. Moreover, the following bounds hold:

(8.4)

\displaystyle\kappa=2h(T)\left(1+O(|\log T|^{-1})\right),\quad|{\partial}_{t}\mathcal{P}_{1}[h](t)|\leq C\frac{|\log T|^{1-\sigma}\left(\log(|\log T|)\right)^{2}}{|\log(T-t)|^{3-\sigma}},

\displaystyle|{\partial}_{t}^{2}\mathcal{P}_{1}[h](t)|\leq C\frac{|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{3}(T-t)},\quad\|{\partial}_{t}\mathcal{P}_{2}[h]\|_{\Theta,\varpi}\leq C\left(T^{\frac{1}{2}+\sigma-\Theta}+\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}\right),

\displaystyle[{\partial}_{t}\mathcal{P}_{2}[h]]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi}\leq C\left(|\log T|^{\varpi-3}T^{\flat-m-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+T^{\Theta}\frac{\log|\log T|}{|\log T|}\|h(\cdot)-h(T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}+[h]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi-1}\right).

The dealing of
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system is similar as the one of
\lambda
-
\gamma
system as their leading non-local operator turn out to be the same. Indeed, from Section
9
, we have:

Proposition 8.2
.

For
|f(t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)
, when
T>0
is sufficiently small, there exist two operators
\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}
and
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}
such that
p_{1}=\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]:[-T,T]\to\mathbb{C}
satisfies the nonlocal equation

\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds+2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}}+\Gamma_{4}[p_{0}]{\mathbf{c}}=f(t)+\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f](t),\quad t\in[0,T]

with

|R_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}(T-t)^{\alpha_{1}}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}

and

\left|\frac{d}{dt}R_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}(T-t)^{\alpha_{1}-1}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

The function
\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]
can be decomposed as
\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]=p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}
. Here
p_{0,1}
and
p_{1,1}
satisfy the following properties

\left|p_{1,0}(t)\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|},

\left|p_{1,1}(t)\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}|\log T|^{k-1}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}},

\left|\dot{p}_{1,1}(t)\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}(T-t)}

and

\left|\ddot{p}_{1,1}(t)\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}(T-t)^{2}}.

Here
k\in(0,1)
,
\alpha_{1}\in(0,1/3)
and

\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}:=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left|\lambda_{*}^{-\Theta}(t)f(t)\right|.

8.2.
The inner-outer gluing system

Because of the refined linear theories for inner problems (Proposition
7.3
, Proposition
7.6
),
\lambda
-
\gamma
system (Proposition
8.1
) and
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system (Proposition
8.2
), we need to further decompose inner problems (
5.1
)
1
-(
5.1
)
2
into three pieces. We search for

\Phi^{*}_{W}+\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}+\Phi^{\dagger}=\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}}

with

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{*}_{W}=

\displaystyle~\Delta_{y}\Phi^{*}_{W}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}W\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}\Phi^{*}_{W}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}\Phi^{*}_{W}\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}W

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}

\displaystyle~+\mathbf{R_{{\mathbf{c}}}}[\Psi]+\sum_{j=1,2}\hat{c}_{1j}\mathcal{Z}_{1,j}w_{\rho}^{2}\quad\mbox{ in }~~\mathcal{D}_{2R},

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}=

\displaystyle~\Delta_{y}\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}W\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}W

(8.5)

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}

\displaystyle~+\mathbf{R_{0}}[\Psi]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}^{(1)}[\Psi]\Big]+\sum_{j=1,2}\tilde{c}_{0j}Z_{0,j}w_{\rho}^{2}+\sum_{j=1,2}c_{1j}Z_{1,j}w_{\rho}^{2}\quad\mbox{ in }~~\mathcal{D}_{2R},

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{\dagger}=

\displaystyle~\Delta_{y}\Phi^{\dagger}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}W\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}\Phi^{\dagger}-2{\partial}_{y_{1}}\Phi^{\dagger}\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}W

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{2}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\left(\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](\lambda y+\xi,t)-\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right)\Big]-\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}^{(1)}[\Psi]\Big]

\displaystyle~+\mathbf{R_{{\mathbf{c}}}}[\Psi]+\mathbf{R_{0}}[\Psi]+\lambda^{2}\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\quad\mbox{ in }~~\mathcal{D}_{2R},

where

(8.6)

\displaystyle\mathbf{R_{{\mathbf{c}}}}[\Psi]:=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\Bigg[\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\lambda\Pi_{W}\left[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right]\cdot\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}\Bigg]\frac{w_{\rho}^{2}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}}{{\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}\mathcal{Z}^{2}_{1,1}}}

\displaystyle+\lambda\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}\Bigg[\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}i\lambda\Pi_{W}\left[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right]\cdot\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}\Bigg]\frac{w_{\rho}^{2}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}}{{\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}\mathcal{Z}^{2}_{1,2}}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{R_{0}}[\Psi]:=

\displaystyle\lambda\mathcal{R}_{0}\Bigg[\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\lambda\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\left[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right]\cdot Z_{0,1}\Bigg]\frac{w_{\rho}^{2}Z_{0,1}}{{\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}Z^{2}_{0,1}}}

\displaystyle+\lambda\mathcal{R}_{0}\Bigg[\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}i\lambda\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\left[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right]\cdot Z_{0,2}\Bigg]\frac{w_{\rho}^{2}Z_{0,2}}{{\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}Z^{2}_{0,2}}}

with the operators
\mathcal{R}_{0}
and
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}
given in Proposition
8.1
and Proposition
8.2
, respectively; for
j=1,2
,

(8.7)

\displaystyle\hat{c}_{1j}=\hat{c}_{1j}\Bigg[\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}+\mathbf{R_{{\mathbf{c}}}}[\Psi]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\tilde{c}_{0j}=\tilde{c}_{0j}\Bigg[\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}+\mathbf{R_{0}}[\Psi]\Bigg]

are given in Proposition
7.6
and Proposition
7.3
, respectively; the term
\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}^{(1)}[\Psi]\Big]
denotes the part of mode
1
in the projection of
Q_{-\gamma}\left(\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](\lambda y+\xi,t)-\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right)
onto
W^{\perp}
,

(8.8)

c_{1j}=\frac{\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\left(\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]+\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\right)\cdot Z_{1,j}}{\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}w_{\rho}^{2}Z_{1,j}^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}:=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\left(({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)W

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta_{1}}{r}\Bigg[\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]\times\sin^{2}w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)W.

We will solve (
8.5
)
1
and (
8.5
)
2
with orthogonality conditions imposed at corresponding modes

\hat{c}_{1j}=0,\quad\tilde{c}_{0j}=0,\quad c_{1j}=0,

and solve (
8.5
)
3
(consisting of components both on
W^{\perp}
and in the
W
-direction) without orthogonality.

\bullet
In the inner problems (supported in
B_{2R}
) with orthogonality condition imposed, by (
A.7
), one has

\displaystyle~\left|\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda^{2}|Q_{-\gamma}\mathcal{R}_{U}|+\lambda^{2}\Big|\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(\mathcal{R}_{*})-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{2}\Bigg|\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\Big(\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1})+\eta_{1}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]\Big)\Big]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{1+\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta+1}+\lambda_{*}^{2}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\langle\rho\rangle^{-1}

\displaystyle~+\Bigg[\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta+1}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-2\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta+1}+\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta+1}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta_{1}}\bigg),

and

\displaystyle~\left|\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])-\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}\Big]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda^{2}\Big|Q_{-\gamma}(\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)})\Big|+\lambda^{2}\Big|\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(\mathcal{R}_{*})\Big]\Big|

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{2}\Bigg|\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\Big(\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1})+\eta_{1}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]\Big)\Big]\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta+1}

\displaystyle~+\Bigg[\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta+1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-2\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-\delta}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta+1}+\lambda_{*}^{2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta+1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}.

We thus need following restrictions on the parameters after simplification by (
8.1
)

(8.9)

\displaystyle 3\Theta-\nu-\beta(a-2)>0,\quad 1-\nu-\beta(a-2)>0,

\displaystyle 2\Theta+1-\nu-\beta(a-\delta)>0,\quad 5\Theta-\nu-\beta(a-1-\delta)>0

so that above terms have finite
\|\cdot\|_{\nu,a}
-norm. Next, we estimate by Lemma
2.3
