\displaystyle\left|\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]\right|,~\left|\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\Big]\right|\lesssim\lambda_{*}\langle y\rangle^{-2}.

So we need

(8.10)

1-\nu-\beta(a-2)>0.

Recall (
5.2
) and (
4.14
). We estimate

(8.11)

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}\Big|,\quad\Big|\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big|\nabla_{y}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\Big|\Big|\nabla_{y}\big(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}+\eta_{1}\Phi_{*}\big)\Big|

\displaystyle+\left|-\eta_{R}\lambda^{2}\dot{\gamma}J_{z}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\eta_{R}(\lambda\dot{\xi}+\lambda\dot{\lambda}y)\cdot\nabla_{y}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\right|

\displaystyle+\left|{\partial}_{y_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{y_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\bigg[\lambda_{*}^{\nu}R^{\sigma_{*}(5-a)}\langle y\rangle^{-4}\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)\lambda_{*}(t)\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\bigg]

\displaystyle\times\Big(\lambda_{*}(t)+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}\langle y\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}\langle y\rangle^{-\delta-1}\Big)

\displaystyle+(\lambda_{*}^{1+\nu}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta+\nu})R^{\sigma_{*}(5-a)}\langle y\rangle^{-3}\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{2\nu}R^{2\sigma_{*}(5-a)}\langle y\rangle^{-8}\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)^{2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(0)\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)\|\Psi\|^{2}_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha},

and thus need

(8.12)

\displaystyle\Theta-\beta\sigma_{*}(5-a)>0,\quad\nu-2\beta\sigma_{*}(5-a)>0,

where parts of the restrictions are in (
8.9
) already.

From Proposition
8.1
and Proposition
8.2
, we have

(8.13)

\displaystyle|\mathbf{R_{0}}[\Psi]|\lesssim

\displaystyle(\lambda_{*}(t))^{1+m+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}}\langle\rho\rangle^{-5}

\displaystyle|\mathbf{R_{{\mathbf{c}}}}[\Psi]|\lesssim

\displaystyle(\lambda_{*}(t))^{1+\alpha_{1}+\Theta}\langle\rho\rangle^{-5}

with sufficient decay in space-time.

\bullet
In (
8.5
)
3
without orthogonality imposed on the RHS, we notice that the term

\lambda^{2}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\left(\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](\lambda y+\xi,t)-\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right)\Big]-\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}^{(1)}[\Psi]\Big]

has no mode
1
on
W^{\perp}
. In order for it to be small such that
\Phi^{\dagger}
, solved from non-orthogonal versions of Proposition
7.4
, Proposition
7.8
(in the
W
-direction), Proposition
7.2
(on
W^{\perp}
) and Remark
7.5.1
, stays within the space with the
\|\cdot\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}
-topology, we need

(8.14)

1+\Theta+\alpha\beta-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a),\quad 0<\alpha<1.

Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|\lambda^{2}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\left(\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](\lambda y+\xi,t)-\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right)\Big]-\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}^{(1)}[\Psi]\Big]\bigg|\lesssim\lambda_{*}\langle y\rangle^{\alpha-2}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}R^{-\alpha}\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}

where we have used Lemma
2.3
and

|\nabla_{x}\Psi(x,t)-\nabla_{x}\Psi(q,t)|\lesssim|x-q|^{\alpha}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(\lambda_{*}R)^{-\alpha}\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

The necessity of restriction (
8.14
) is clear for all the modes except for the mode
0
in the
W
-direction. For the mode
0
in the
W
-direction, we can estimate

\displaystyle~\bigg|\lambda^{2}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\left(\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](\lambda y+\xi,t)-\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi](q,t)\right)\Big]-\lambda^{2}\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}^{(1)}[\Psi]\Big]\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}\langle y\rangle^{\alpha-2}\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}R^{-\alpha}\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{1+\Theta}\langle y\rangle^{-\ell}R^{\ell-2}\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}

thanks to the support of the inner problem. Here
\ell>3/2
and is close to
3/2
, and
\alpha\in(0,1)
and is close to
1
. So we need

1+\Theta-\beta(\ell-2)>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a),

which is true if (
8.14
) holds.

For the other part
\lambda^{2}\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}
, by definitions (
4.10
) and (
4.12
), we first observe that it is in modes
\pm 1
,
\pm 3
(in the
W
-direction), and also

|\lambda^{2}\mathcal{R}^{\dagger}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta}\langle y\rangle^{-3-\delta}.

Then from Proposition
7.5
and Proposition
7.7
, we require

(8.15)

3\Theta-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a)

so that
\|\Phi^{\dagger}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}<+\infty.

For
\mathbf{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[\Psi]
and
\mathbf{R}_{0}[\Psi]
, since they are respectively in mode
\pm 1
of the
W
-direction and in mode
0
on
W^{\perp}
, we apply the non-orthogonal part of Proposition
7.2
and Proposition
7.5
. By (
8.13
), we need the restrictions (
8.21
) and (
8.23
) and so that
\Phi^{\dagger}
survives in the desired topology.

\bullet
We now consider the outer problem

(8.16)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\partial}_{t}\Psi=\Delta_{x}\Psi+\mathcal{G}~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{2}\times(0,T),\\
&\Psi(x,0)=Z^{*}(x)+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\mathscr{C}_{k}\mathscr{Z}_{k}(x)~&\mbox{ in }~{\mathbb{R}}^{2},\end{aligned}\right.

where
\mathcal{G}=\mathcal{G}[\Phi_{W},\Phi_{W^{\perp}},\Psi,p_{0},p_{1},\xi,Z^{*}]
is the RHS in the outer problem (
5.1
)
3
, and
\mathscr{C}_{k}
will be chosen such that we have desired vanishing (
7.5
). The outer problem can be formulated as a fixed-point problem

\mathcal{T}_{{\rm out}}[\Psi]=\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\bullet\mathcal{G}+\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ\left(Z^{*}+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\mathscr{C}_{k}\mathscr{Z}_{k}\right).

For the RHS
\mathcal{G}
given in (
5.1
)
3
, by (
A.7
) and (
A.8
), we have

\displaystyle~\left|(1-\eta_{R})S[U_{*}]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~(1-\eta_{R})\Bigg|\Big[\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\mathcal{R}_{U}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)}+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1})+\eta_{1}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]\Big]\Bigg|

\displaystyle~+(1-\eta_{R})|\mathcal{R}_{*}|+|E_{\eta_{1}}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\Big(\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\Big)\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\lesssim r\}}+\Big(\lambda_{*}^{-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-1}+|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|\langle\rho\rangle^{-1}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\lesssim r\lesssim 1\}}

\displaystyle~+\Bigg[1+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta-2}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-2\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-\delta}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\lambda_{*}R\lesssim r\lesssim 1\}}+1.

In order for above terms to have finite
\|\cdot\|_{**}
-norm, following restrictions should be imposed on parameters

(8.17)

\displaystyle\sigma_{0}>0,\quad 3\Theta-1\geq 0,\quad 2\Theta-1+\delta\beta>0.

Also

\displaystyle\left|(1-\eta_{R})\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\right|\lesssim\frac{\lambda_{*}(t)}{r^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq\lambda_{*}R\}}\lesssim T^{\epsilon}\varrho_{2}

for some
\epsilon>0
provided
\sigma_{0}>0
.
For the remaining terms, we estimate

\displaystyle\left|Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\Delta_{x}\eta_{R}+2\nabla_{x}\eta_{R}\cdot\nabla_{x}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})-Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\partial_{t}\eta_{R}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big[\lambda_{*}^{\nu-2}R^{-a}+\lambda_{*}^{\nu}R^{2-a}(T-t)^{-1}\big]\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{r\sim\lambda_{*}R\}},

\displaystyle\left|(1-\eta_{R})\Big[-\eta_{R}\dot{\gamma}J_{z}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\eta_{R}(\lambda^{-1}\dot{\xi}+\lambda^{-1}\dot{\lambda}y)\cdot\nabla_{y}(Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W}+Q_{\gamma}\Phi_{W^{\perp}})\Big]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big(\lambda_{*}^{\nu-1}R^{2-a}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta+\nu-2}R^{1-a}\Big)\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq\lambda_{*}R\}},

and by (
4.14
), one has

\displaystyle~(1-\eta_{R})\bigg|-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}(U_{*}-U)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)

\displaystyle~\qquad\qquad-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(U_{*}-U)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~(1-\eta_{R})\Big|\nabla_{x}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\Big|\Big|\nabla_{x}\big(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}+\eta_{1}\Phi_{*}\big)\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\bigg[\lambda_{*}^{\nu-1}R^{1-a}\mathbf{1}_{\{r\sim\lambda_{*}R\}}\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(0)\mathbf{1}_{\{r\geq\lambda_{*}R\}}\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}\bigg]

\displaystyle~\times\Big(1+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}\langle y\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\langle y\rangle^{-\delta-1}\Big).

Also, we have

\displaystyle~(1-\eta_{R})\left|-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~(1-\eta_{R})\Big|\nabla_{x}\Big(\eta_{R}Q_{\gamma}(\Phi_{W}+\Phi_{W^{\perp}})+\Psi\Big)\Big|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}^{2\nu-2}\langle y\rangle^{2-2a}\Big(\|\Phi_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\Big)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|y|\sim R\}}+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(0)\|\Psi\|^{2}_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}.

To bound above terms in the
\|\cdot\|_{**}
-topology, we need

(8.18)

\displaystyle\nu-2+a\beta>\Theta-1+\beta,\quad\nu-1-\beta(2-a)>\Theta-1+\beta,

\displaystyle 3\Theta+\nu-2-\beta(1-a)>\Theta-1+\beta,\quad\nu-1-\beta(1-a)>\Theta-1+\beta,

\displaystyle\nu+\Theta-2+\beta(1+a)>\Theta-1+\beta,\quad 2\nu-2-\beta(2-2a)>\Theta-1+\beta.

For the Cauchy integrals
\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ\left(Z^{*}+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\mathscr{C}_{k}\mathscr{Z}_{k}\right)
, we claim

\left|\Gamma_{{\mathbb{R}}^{2}}\circ\left(Z^{*}+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\mathscr{C}_{k}\mathscr{Z}_{k}\right)\right|\lesssim 1

since
\|Z^{*}+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\mathscr{C}_{k}\mathscr{Z}_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1
. Indeed,
\mathscr{C}_{k}
is chosen such that the vanishing (
7.6
) holds, so the Cauchy integrals have the same control as the space-time convolutions estimated above. In fact, one has better estimate
\sum_{k=1}^{5}|\mathscr{C}_{k}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-\Theta-1}(0)R^{-1}(0)|\log T|^{-1}
that can be derived similar to
[
17
, Proposition A.1]
.

8.3.
Orthogonal equations

We first employ Proposition
8.1
and Proposition
8.2
to derive restrictions on constants, measuring weighted topologies, that ensure the implementation of the gluing process, and then analyze the remainder terms neglected in Section
6
.

\bullet
For the orthogonal equation of
p_{0}
:

\tilde{c}_{0j}=0,

we apply Proposition
8.1
with

h[\Psi]:=\left[{\partial}_{x_{1}}\Psi_{1}+{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Psi_{2}+i\left({\partial}_{x_{1}}\Psi_{2}-{\partial}_{x_{2}}\Psi_{1}\right)\right](q,t).

The vanishing and Hölder properties (
8.3
) are exactly the ones inherited from the weighted topology (
7.4
) for the outer problem, namely

\displaystyle|h[\Psi](t)-h[\Psi](T)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t),

\displaystyle\frac{|h[\Psi](t)-h[\Psi](s)|}{|t-s|^{\alpha/2}}\lesssim\lambda_{*}(t)^{\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)}\mbox{ \ for \ }|t-s|<\frac{T-t}{4}.

So it is then natural to choose in the
[\cdot]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi-1}
-seminorm

m=\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta).

Then in the last line of (
8.4
),
\flat-m-\frac{\alpha}{2}>0
. In order for both
\|h[\Psi](\cdot)-h[\Psi](T)\|_{\Theta,\varpi-1}
,
[h[\Psi]]_{\frac{\alpha}{2},m,\varpi-1}
to be finite, we need

\varpi-1-2\Theta<0,\quad\varpi-1-2m<0.

Also the assumption
m\leq\Theta-\frac{\alpha}{2}
in Proposition
8.1
implies

\beta\leq 1/2,

which is in the desired self-similar regime as we require before.
Recall the estimate of
\mathcal{R}_{0}[h[\Psi]]
. We require

m+(1+\alpha_{0})\frac{\alpha}{2}>\Theta,

namely,

0<\alpha_{0}<1/2,\quad 2\beta-1+\alpha_{0}>0,

so that the vanishing order of
\mathcal{R}_{0}[h[\Psi]]
as
t\to T
is faster than the leading part
h[\Psi]
itself.

Under the paramter assumption above, the equation for
p_{0}
will be solve by Proposition
8.1
and the solution
p_{0}
satisfies the estimate in (
8.4
). We can then find a solution
\xi
to the orthogonal equation

c_{1j}=0

with the estimate

(8.19)

|\dot{\xi}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-1}

provided

2\nu-2\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a)-3\Theta>0.

We then conclude that with above choices of
m,~\alpha_{0},~\frac{\alpha}{2},~\varpi
, the remainder gains smallness

(8.20)

|\mathcal{R}_{0}[h[\Psi]](t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta+\sigma_{1}}

compare to the leading part
h[\Psi]
itself, where

0<\sigma_{1}<m+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}-\Theta.

We put the remainder
\mathcal{R}_{0}[h[\Psi]]
, i.e.,
\mathbf{R_{0}}[\Psi]
defined in (
8.6
), in the non-orthogonal inner problem, where the extra smallness measured above by
\sigma_{1}
is crucial to control the non-orthogonal part. Indeed, from the version of linear theory without orthogonality condition imposed at mode
0
on
W^{\perp}
in Proposition
7.2
, we need

(8.21)

\displaystyle 1+\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a).

In summary, the restrictions on the constants needed when dealing with the reduced problem are given by

(8.22)

\displaystyle 0<\beta<\frac{1}{2},\quad 0<\alpha_{0}<\frac{1}{2},\quad 2\beta-1+\alpha_{0}>0,\quad 2\nu-2\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a)-3\Theta>0,

\displaystyle 1+\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a).

\bullet
By the same reasoning as in
\lambda
-
\gamma
system,
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system will not be completely solved. Instead, the term
\mathbf{R}_{\mathbf{c}}
defined in (
8.6
) that produces the remainder
R_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]
will be put in the piece of inner problem with no orthogonality condition imposed. Recall the non-orthogonal linear theory Proposition
7.5
for modes
\pm 1
in the
W
-direction. Therefore, from its vanishing bound, one requires

1+\Theta+\alpha_{1}-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a).

A more convenient way to achieve above restriction is to assume

(8.23)

\alpha_{1}>\alpha\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{2}-\frac{1}{2}+\beta\right)

since it will be satisfied provided (
8.22
) and (
8.23
) hold.

We now analyze those terms that we neglect when deriving the leading dynamics of the modulation parameters. We recall (
6.4
) and consider the remainders for the mode 0 on
W^{\perp}
:

\displaystyle\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi]\Big]+\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}

\displaystyle-\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big]-\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\Big((\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}]\Big)\Big].

In
\mathcal{D}_{2R}
, one has

\displaystyle~\left|\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}(S[U_{*}])\Big]-\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\Big((\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}]\Big)\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},0}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left|\left(\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\left(\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}+\mathcal{R}_{*,1}\right)\Big]\right)_{\mathbb{C},0}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1}+1+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta-2}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-2\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle~+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-\delta},

where we have used (
4.20
), (
6.2
) and (
A.7
). Also, it follows from (
7.4
) that

\Big|Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi-Z_{0}^{*}(q)]\Big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1},

and
\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}}
is estimated in (
8.11
).

We next consider the remainders in mode 1 on
W^{\perp}
. To estimate

\Pi_{W^{\perp}}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W^{\perp}},

we recall (
6.2
). Similar to (
A.7
), one can verify that

(8.24)

\displaystyle\Big|\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}W\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg[1+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta-2}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-2\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-4-\delta}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\}},

\displaystyle\Big|\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{1}\Big|,~\Big|\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{2}\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg[1+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta-2}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-2\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-\delta}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\}}.

The estimates for remainders in modes
\pm 1
in the
W
-direction

\Pi_{W}\Big[Q_{-\gamma}\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{*}\Big]+\mathcal{H}_{{\rm in}}^{W}

are similar (cf. (
8.11
) and (
8.24
)). Recall that the outer problem will be solved within the weighted space (
7.4
) with the vanishings (
7.5
). Thanks to this, in the reduced problem for
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\lambda}{\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Psi(q,t)]\cdot W\right)\sin we^{i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg|\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\Big[\partial_{x_{1}}\Psi_{3}(q,t)\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}\Psi_{3}(q,t)\sin\theta\Big]e^{i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg|\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{0}^{+\infty}w_{\rho}\sin^{2}w\Big[\Big(\partial_{x_{1}}\Psi_{3}(q,t)-\partial_{x_{1}}\Psi_{3}(q,T)\Big)\cos\theta+\Big(\partial_{x_{2}}\Psi_{3}(q,t)-\partial_{x_{2}}\Psi_{3}(q,T)\Big)\sin\theta\Big]e^{i\theta}\rho d\rho d\theta\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha},

where we have used Lemma
2.3
. This term appears as part of
f(t)
in Proposition
8.2
.

From the estimates above, we see that, under the final choice of constants (
8.29
), the remainders in the orthogonal equations
\tilde{c}_{0j}=c_{1j}=\hat{c}_{1j}=0
are indeed of smaller order than the main order taken into account when deriving the leading dynamics in Section
6
.

8.4.
Solving the full system: Proof of Theorem
1.2

We now formulate the full gluing system (
8.5
) & (
8.16
) together with the orthogonal equations

(8.25)

\tilde{c}_{0j}=c_{1j}=\hat{c}_{1j}=0

as a fixed-point problem. Here the definitions for
\hat{c}_{1j}
,
\tilde{c}_{0j}
,
c_{1j}
are given in (
8.7
) and (
8.8
).

We will solve the outer problem (
8.16
) in the space

(8.26)

X_{\Psi}:=\Big\{\Psi=(\Psi_{1},\Psi_{2},\Psi_{3}):\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}<+\infty,\ \Psi_{j}(q,T)={\partial}_{x_{1}}\Psi_{3}(q,T)={\partial}_{x_{2}}\Psi_{3}(q,T)=0,~j=1,2,3\Big\}

and the inner problems (
8.5
)
1
-(
8.5
)
3
for
\Phi^{*}_{W}
,
\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}
and
\Phi^{\dagger}
all in the space

(8.27)

\displaystyle X_{\Phi^{*}_{W}}:=\Big\{\Phi^{*}_{W},~\nabla_{y}\Phi^{*}_{W}\in L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D}_{2R}):\|\Phi^{*}_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}<+\infty\Big\},

\displaystyle X_{\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}}:=\Big\{\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}},~\nabla_{y}\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}\in L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D}_{2R}):\|\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}<+\infty\Big\},

\displaystyle X_{\Phi^{\dagger}}:=\Big\{\Phi^{\dagger},~\nabla_{y}\Phi^{\dagger}\in L^{\infty}(\mathcal{D}_{2R}):\|\Phi^{\dagger}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}<+\infty\Big\}.

We consider the modulation problem (
8.25
). For
\tilde{c}_{0j}=0
, Proposition
8.1
gives an approximate inverse
\mathcal{P}
of the operator
\mathcal{B}_{0}
, so that for given
h(t)
satisfying (
8.3
),
p_{0}:=\mathcal{P}\left[h\right]

satisfies

\mathcal{B}_{0}[p_{0}]=h+\mathcal{R}_{0}[h]\quad\text{ in }~[0,T]

for a small remainder
\mathcal{R}_{0}[h]
. Moreover, estimates (
8.4
) for

p_{0,1}:=\mathcal{P}_{1}[h]+\mathcal{P}_{2}[h]

in Proposition
8.1
lead us to define the space

X_{p_{0}}:=\{p_{0,1}\in C([-T,T;\mathbb{C}])\cap C^{1}([-T,T;\mathbb{C}])\ :\ p_{0,1}(T)=0,\ \|p_{0,1}\|_{*,3-\sigma_{0}}<+\infty\}

for some
\sigma_{0}\in(0,1)
, where we represent
p_{0}
by the pair
(\kappa,p_{0,1})
in the form
p_{0}=p_{0,\kappa}+p_{0,1}
, and the
\|\cdot\|_{*,3-\sigma_{0}}
-seminorm is defined by

\|f\|_{*,3-\sigma_{0}}:=\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}|\log(T-t)|^{3-\sigma_{0}}|\dot{f}(t)|.

For
c_{1j}=0
, we define the space for
\xi(t)
as

X_{\xi}=\left\{\xi\in C^{1}((0,T);{\mathbb{R}}^{2})~:~\dot{\xi}(T)=0,~\|\xi\|_{X_{\xi}}<+\infty\right\}

where

\|\xi\|_{X_{\xi}}=\|\xi\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T)}+\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}\lambda_{*}^{-\sigma_{1}}(t)|\dot{\xi}(t)|

for some
\sigma_{1}\in(0,3\Theta-1)
.

Similarly, for
\hat{c}_{1j}=0
, Proposition
8.2
concerns the solvability of
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system, i.e.,
p_{1}=-2\big[\lambda(c_{1}+ic_{2})\big]^{\prime}
, up to a small remainder
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f](t)
. The control of the resolution

p_{1}=\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]=p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}

for the modified non-local problem motivates us to solve
p_{1}
in the space

X_{p_{1}}:=\{p_{1}\in C([-T,T;\mathbb{C}])\cap C^{1}([-T,T;\mathbb{C}])\ :\ p_{1}(T)=0,\ \|p_{1}\|_{\Theta,1}<+\infty\},

where the norm above is defined in (
8.2
).

We define
\mathfrak{X}:=X_{\Psi}\times X_{\Phi^{*}_{W}}\times X_{\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}}\times X_{\Phi^{\dagger}}\times\mathbb{C}\times X_{p_{0}}\times X_{\xi}\times X_{p_{1}}
and take a closed subset
\mathfrak{B}\subset\mathfrak{X}
for which

(\Psi,\Phi^{*}_{W},~\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}},~\Phi^{\dagger},~\kappa,~p_{0,1},~\xi,~p_{1})\in\mathfrak{B}

satisfies

\displaystyle\|\Psi\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}+\|\Phi^{*}_{W}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}+\|\Phi^{\dagger}\|_{{\rm in},\sigma_{*},\nu,a}\leq 1,

\displaystyle|\kappa-\kappa_{0}|\leq|\log T|^{-1},\quad\kappa_{0}=\mathop{\rm div}Z^{*}(q)+i{\rm{curl}}Z^{*}(q),

\displaystyle\|p_{0,1}\|_{*,3-\sigma_{0}}\leq C_{0}|\log T|^{1-\sigma_{0}}(\log(|\log T|))^{2},\quad\|\xi\|_{X_{\xi}}+\|p_{1}\|_{\Theta,\sigma_{2}}\leq 1

for a sufficiently large constant
C_{0}
. Then we define an operator
\mathfrak{F}
which returns the solution from
\mathfrak{B}
to
\mathfrak{X}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{F}:\mathfrak{B}\subset\mathfrak{X}~\rightarrow

\displaystyle\mathfrak{X}

\displaystyle v~\mapsto

\displaystyle\mathfrak{F}(v):=\Big(\mathfrak{F}_{\Psi}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{\Phi^{*}_{W}}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{\Phi^{\dagger}}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{\kappa}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{p_{0,1}}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{\xi}(v),~\mathfrak{F}_{p_{1}}(v)\Big).

Here the operator
\mathfrak{F}_{\Psi}
corresponds to the outer problem (
8.16
) with linear theory given by Proposition
7.1
. The operators
\mathfrak{F}_{\Phi^{*}_{W}}
,
\mathfrak{F}_{\Phi^{*}_{W^{\perp}}}
,
\mathfrak{F}_{\Phi^{\dagger}}
handle respectively three pieces of inner problems (
8.5
)
1
, (
8.5
)
2
, (
8.5
)
3
, and their linear theories are given in Proposition
7.8
and Proposition
7.2
. The operators
\mathfrak{F}_{\kappa}
,
\mathfrak{F}_{p_{0,1}}
deal with the
\lambda
-
\gamma
system from
\tilde{c}_{0j}=0
whose linear theory is in Proposition
8.1
. The operator
\mathfrak{F}_{\xi}
concerns
c_{1j}=0
yielding a first order ODE for
\xi
. The operator
\mathfrak{F}_{p_{1}}
is related to the
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system from
\hat{c}_{1j}=0
with linear theory given by Proposition
8.2
.

The property that
\mathfrak{F}:\mathfrak{B}\rightarrow\mathfrak{B}
follows from those estimates in Section
8.2
and Section
8.3
under all the restrictions (
8.1
), (
8.9
), (
8.10
), (
8.12
), (
8.14
), (
8.15
), (
8.17
), (
8.18
), (
8.22
) and (
8.23
) for the constants. These can be simplified as

(8.28)

\displaystyle 0<\nu<1,\quad 2<a<3,\quad 1/3<\Theta<\beta<1/2,

\displaystyle 1-\nu-\beta(a-2)>0,\quad 0<\sigma_{*}<1,

\displaystyle\Theta-\beta\sigma_{*}(5-a)>0,\quad\nu-2\beta\sigma_{*}(5-a)>0,

\displaystyle 3\Theta-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a),\quad\nu-2+a\beta>\Theta-1+\beta,

\displaystyle 0<\alpha<1,\quad\alpha_{0}\approx 1/2,\quad 0<\alpha_{1}<1/3,\quad\alpha_{1}>\alpha\left(\frac{\alpha_{0}}{2}-\frac{1}{2}+\beta\right),

\displaystyle 1+\Theta-\alpha(1-\beta)+\frac{(1+\alpha_{0})\alpha}{2}-2\beta>\nu-\sigma_{*}\beta(5-a),

where we take
\delta=\frac{999}{1000}\approx 1
,
\alpha_{0}=\frac{49}{100}\approx 1/2
. With the aid of Mathematica, the system (
8.28
) admits a valid choice of constants:

(8.29)

\displaystyle\frac{37}{60}<\nu<\frac{5}{8},\quad\frac{209-120\nu}{45}<a<3,\quad\beta=\frac{3}{8},\quad\Theta=\frac{11}{30},

\displaystyle\frac{8\nu-\frac{14}{5}}{15-3a}<\sigma_{*}<\frac{4\nu}{15-3a},\quad\alpha=\frac{99}{100},\quad\frac{297}{2500}<\alpha_{1}<\frac{1}{3}.

The compactness of the operator
\mathfrak{F}
can be proved by suitable variants of (
8.29
). Indeed, one can vary slightly the constants such that all the restrictions in (
8.28
) still hold, and get (
8.29
) with the weighted norms measured by the new constants with the closed ball
\mathfrak{B}
remains the same. For instance, for fixed
\Theta^{\prime},~\alpha^{\prime}
close to
\Theta,\alpha
, one can show that if
v\in\mathfrak{B}
, then

\|\mathfrak{F}_{\Psi}(v)\|_{\sharp,\Theta^{\prime},\alpha^{\prime}}\leq CT^{\epsilon^{\prime}}

for some constants
C,~\epsilon^{\prime}>0
.
Moreover, one can show that for
\alpha^{\prime}>\alpha
and
\Theta^{\prime}-\Theta>2(\alpha^{\prime}-\alpha)
, one has a compact embedding in the sense that if a sequence
\{\Psi_{k}\}_{k}
is bounded in the
\|\cdot\|_{\sharp,\Theta^{\prime},\alpha^{\prime}}
-norm, then there exists a subsequence that converges in the
\|\cdot\|_{\sharp,\Theta,\alpha}
-norm. The compactness thus follows directly from Arzelà–Ascoli’s theorem by a standard diagonal argument. The compactness of the rest operators can be proved in a similar manner. Therefore, the Schauder fixed-point theorem implies the existence of a desired solution. The proof of Theorem
1.2
is complete.

9.
Non-local
c_{1}
-
c_{2}
system

In this section, we solve the equation for
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
defined in Section
6

(9.1)

\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds+2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}}+\Gamma_{4}[p_{0}]{\mathbf{c}}=f(t).

Recall from Section
6
that
p_{1}=-2(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime},\quad p_{0}=-2(\dot{\lambda}+i\lambda\dot{\gamma})e^{i\gamma}
,
{\mathbf{c}}(t):=c_{1}(t)+ic_{2}(t)
and
f(t)
is a smooth function satisfying the condition
|f(t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)
. For simplicity, we will use the notations in this following:
{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}
,
{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}
and
p_{1,0}(t)=\left(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}\right)^{\prime}
,
p_{1,1}(t)=\left(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}\right)^{\prime}
.

9.1.
The construction of a solution

According to the computations in Section
6
, (
9.1
) can be approximated by the following equation

\mathcal{P}_{1}(t)+\frac{2}{3}\mathcal{P}_{0}(t){\mathbf{c}}=f(t)\text{ in }[0,T),

so we need to construct an operator
\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}
which assigns
p_{1}=\mathcal{P}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f]
to a function
f
in a suitable class such that

(9.2)

\mathcal{P}_{1}(t)+\frac{2}{3}\mathcal{P}_{0}(t){\mathbf{c}}=f(t)+\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f](t)\text{ in }[0,T),

so that
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f](t)
is a suitable small remainder term. The first approximation is the following function

\displaystyle\lambda{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}=

\displaystyle(T-t)^{\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\log(T-t)}\int_{t}^{T}(T-\tau)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6}\log(T-\tau)}\left(\frac{f(\tau)}{2\log(T-\tau)}-\frac{1}{2\log^{2}(T-\tau)}\int_{\tau}^{T}\frac{f(s)}{T-s}ds\right)d\tau,

which is a solution of the equation

\mathcal{P}(t):=\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{T-s}ds-p_{1,0}(t)\log(T-t)-\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}=f(t),\quad p_{1,0}(t)=(-2\lambda\mathbf{c}^{(0)})^{\prime}

and it is an approximation to the equation (
9.2
). Observe that we have
|\lambda\mathbf{c}^{(0)}|\leq\frac{\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)|\log T|(T-t)}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}
and
|(\lambda\mathbf{c}^{(0)})^{\prime}|\leq\frac{\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)}{|\log(T-t)|}
since
f(t)
satisfies the condition
|f(t)|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)
.

We look for
p_{1}
with the form
p_{1}=p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}
, where
p_{1,0}(t)=(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)})^{\prime}
and
p_{1}
satisfies the following equation

\mathcal{I}[p_{1,0}]+\mathcal{I}[p_{1,1}]+\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]-\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}\left({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}\right)=f(t)+\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f](t)\text{ for }t\in[0,T].

Here we define
\mathcal{I}[p]=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p(s)}{t-s}ds
, the term
\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]
are small terms defined as follows,

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}[p_{1}]=\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{1}[p_{1}]+\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{2}[p_{1}]{\mathbf{c}}+\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{3}[p_{1}]{\mathbf{c}}-2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}},

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{1}[p_{1}]=-\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds-\int_{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds,

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{2}[p_{1}]=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\right)ds+\int_{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds,

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{3}[p_{1}]=-\frac{2}{3}\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}.

The idea is to decompose
\mathcal{I}[p_{1,1}]
into
\mathcal{I}[p_{1,1}]=S_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]+\mathcal{R}_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]
, then replace the operator
\mathcal{I}[p_{1,1}]
by
S_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]
and try to solve the corresponding equation. If
\alpha_{1}>0
is small, then we can find
p_{1,1}
such that

\mathcal{I}[p_{1,0}]+S_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]+\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]-\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}\left({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}\right)=f(t)\text{ for }t\in[0,T].

This means that we have

\mathcal{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]-\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}\left({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}\right)=f(t)+\mathcal{R}_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]\text{ for }t\in[0,T].

We will prove that

\left|\mathcal{R}_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]\right|\leq C(T-t)^{\Theta+\alpha_{1}}\text{ for }t\in[0,T].

Now we decompose

S_{\alpha_{1}}[g]=\tilde{L}_{0}[g]+\tilde{L}_{1}[g]

where

\tilde{L}_{0}[g](t)=(1-\alpha_{1})|\log(T-t)|g(t)

and
\tilde{L}_{1}[g]
contains all other terms, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{1}[g]

\displaystyle=\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{g(s)}{T-s}ds+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}\frac{g(s)}{t-s}ds-\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\frac{g(s)}{T-s}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}g(s)\left(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\right)ds+(4\log(|\log(T-t)|)-2\log(|\log T|))g(t).

Now we look for a function
g
solving the following problem

S_{\alpha_{1}}[g]-\frac{1}{3}\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{\lambda}g=\tilde{f}(t)\text{ in }[-T,T].

We solve a modified version of this equation. Let
\eta
be a smooth cut-off function such that

\eta(s)=1\text{ for }s\geq 0,\,\,\eta(s)=0\text{ for }s\leq-\frac{1}{4}.

We will find a function
g
such that

(9.3)

\tilde{L}_{0}[g]+\eta(\frac{t}{T})\tilde{L}_{1}[g]-\frac{1}{3}\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{\lambda}g=\tilde{f}(t)\text{ in }[-T,T].

Lemma 9.1
.

When
\alpha_{1}\in(0,\frac{1}{3})
and
T>0
are sufficiently small, there is a linear operator
T_{1}
such that
g=T_{1}[\tilde{f}]
satisfies (
9.3
) and the following estimate holds

\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq C\|\tilde{f}\|_{*,\Theta,k}.

Here the norms are defined by

\|h\|_{*,\Theta,k}=\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-\Theta}|\log(T-t)|^{k}|h(t)|.

Here
k\in(0,1)
.

Let us start with the construction of the linear operator
T_{1}
. We will find an inverse for
\tilde{L}_{0}
, namely given a function
\tilde{f}
, find
g
such that
\tilde{L}_{0}[g]-\frac{1}{3}\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{\lambda}g=\tilde{f}
.
Set
g=(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}
, differentiating this equation we get

\displaystyle-2(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime\prime}-2\frac{1}{(T-t)|\log(T-t)|}(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}+\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{{\mathbf{c}}^{\prime}}{|\log(T-t)|}=\frac{1}{1-\alpha_{1}}\frac{\tilde{f}^{\prime}(t)}{|\log(T-t)|}.

This equation can be rewritten as

\displaystyle-2\left(|\log(T-t)|(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}\right)^{\prime}+\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3(1-\alpha_{1})}{\mathbf{c}}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{1-\alpha_{1}}\tilde{f}^{\prime}(t).

Integrating this equation gives us

\displaystyle 2|\log(T-t)|(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}+\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3(1-\alpha_{1})}\int_{t}^{T}{\mathbf{c}}^{\prime}(s)ds=-\frac{1}{1-\alpha_{1}}\tilde{f}(t),

which is equivalent to

(9.4)

\displaystyle(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{3(1-\alpha_{1})}|\log(T-t)|^{-1}{\mathbf{c}}(t)=\frac{1}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}|\log(T-t)|^{-1}\tilde{f}(t).

Rewrite it as

\displaystyle\left[(T-t)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}(\lambda{\mathbf{c}})\right]^{\prime}=(T-t)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\frac{1}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}|\log(T-t)|^{-1}\tilde{f}(t).

We finally get

(9.5)

\displaystyle\lambda{\mathbf{c}}=(T-t)^{\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\int_{t}^{T}(T-\tau)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-\tau)}{|\log T|}}\frac{1}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}|\log(T-\tau)|^{-1}\tilde{f}(\tau)d\tau.

We denote the the operator which assigns
\tilde{f}(t)
to
(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}
by the formulas (
9.5
) and (
9.4
) as
T_{0}
.

Lemma 9.2
.

Set
\alpha_{1}\in(0,\frac{1}{3})
,
k\in(0,1)
and
(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}:=T_{0}(\tilde{f})
given by (
9.5
). Then the following estimate holds

\|T_{0}[\tilde{f}]\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq\frac{2}{1-\alpha_{1}}\|\tilde{f}\|_{*,\Theta,k}

for a constant
C>0
which is independent of
k
and
T
.

Proof.

We recall that

\|\tilde{f}\|_{*,\Theta,k}=\sup_{t\in[-T,T]}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-\Theta}|\log(T-t)|^{k}|\tilde{f}(t)|.

Then from the formula (
9.5
), we have the following estimate

\displaystyle\left|\lambda{\mathbf{c}}\right|

\displaystyle=(T-t)^{\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\int_{t}^{T}(T-\tau)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-\tau)}{|\log T|}}\frac{1}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}|\log(T-\tau)|^{-1}\tilde{f}(\tau)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}\|\tilde{f}\|_{**,\Theta,k}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}(T-t)^{\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-t)}{|\log T|}}\int_{t}^{T}(T-\tau)^{-\frac{\mathbf{Z}}{6(1-\alpha_{1})}\frac{\log(T-\tau)}{|\log T|}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}|\log T|(T-t)}{2\mathbf{Z}|\log(T-t)|^{k+2}}\|\tilde{f}\|_{**,\Theta,k}.

Using equation (
9.4
), we know that

\displaystyle\left|(-2\lambda{\mathbf{c}})^{\prime}\right|\leq\frac{2(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{(1-\alpha_{1})|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}\|\tilde{f}\|_{**,\Theta,k}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
9.1
:
We construct
g
as a solution of the fixed point problem

g=T_{0}\left[\tilde{f}-\eta(\frac{t}{T})\tilde{L}_{1}[g]\right]

where
T_{0}
is the operator defined in (
9.5
) and
\eta
is the cut-off function. By Lemma
9.2
, we have

\|T_{0}\left[\eta(\frac{t}{T})\tilde{L}_{1}[g]\right]\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq\frac{2}{1-\alpha_{1}}\|\tilde{L}_{1}[g]\|_{*,\Theta,k}.

Let us estimate the different terms in
\tilde{L}_{1}
, which are defined by

\tilde{L}_{1}[g]=\sum_{j=0}^{4}\tilde{L}_{1j}[g]

where

\tilde{L}_{10}[g]=\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{g(s)}{T-s}ds,\quad\tilde{L}_{11}[g]=\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}\frac{g(s)}{t-s}ds,\quad\tilde{L}_{12}[g]=\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\frac{g(s)}{T-s}ds

\tilde{L}_{13}[g]=\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}g(s)\left(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\right)ds,\quad\tilde{L}_{14}[g]=(4\log(|\log(T-t)|)-2\log(|\log T|))g(t).

Then we have the following estimates. First,

\displaystyle|\tilde{L}_{10}[g]|

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(T-s)|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}},

and

\displaystyle|\tilde{L}_{11}[g]|

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}\frac{1}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{\alpha_{1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k}},

therefore

\|\tilde{L}_{10}[g]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{1}{|\log T|}\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1},\quad\|\tilde{L}_{11}[g]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\alpha_{0}\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

Second,

\displaystyle|\tilde{L}_{12}[g]|

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(T-s)|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\frac{1}{T-s}ds

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}\log 2

which implies that

\|\tilde{L}_{12}[g]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{\log 2}{|\log T|}\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

For
\tilde{L}_{13}
we have

\displaystyle|\tilde{L}_{13}[g]|

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}\left(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\right)ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(T-t)\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(T-s)^{2}|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}

and this gives

\|\tilde{L}_{13}[g]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{C}{|\log T|}\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

Finally, we have

\displaystyle|\tilde{L}_{14}[g]|

\displaystyle\leq\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}\frac{\log(|\log(T-t)|)+\log(|\log T|))}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}

and this gives us the estimate

\|\tilde{L}_{14}[g]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{C\log(|\log T|)}{|\log T|}\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

Combine all the estimates above we get

\displaystyle\|T_{0}[\eta(\frac{t}{T})\tilde{L}_{1}[g]]\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq\frac{2}{1-\alpha_{1}}\left(\alpha_{1}+\frac{1}{|\log T|}+\frac{\log|\log T|}{|\log T|}\right)\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

If
0<\alpha_{1}<\frac{1}{3}
is fixed and
T>0
is sufficiently small, then we get

\|g\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq C\|\tilde{f}\|_{*,\Theta,k}.

∎

Let

(9.6)

E(t)=\mathcal{I}[p_{1,0}](t)+\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}-f(t)

and we consider the fixed point problem

(9.7)

p_{1,1}=\mathcal{A}[p_{1,1}]

where

\mathcal{A}[p_{1,1}]=T_{1}[-\eta(\frac{t}{T})E-\tilde{\mathcal{B}}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]].

Then we have

Proposition 9.3
.

When
\alpha\in(0,\frac{1}{3})
and
k\in(0,1)
, there is a function
p_{1,1}
satisfying (
9.7
) satisfying
\|p_{1,1}\|_{*,k+1}\leq C_{0}|\log T|^{k-1}
. Here
C_{0}
is a sufficiently large but fixed constant.

We divide the proof into the following lemmas.

Lemma 9.4
.

For the term
E(t)
defined in (
9.6
), we have

(9.8)

|E(t)|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-t)\right|},\quad-\frac{T}{4}\leq t\leq T.

Proof.

By definition (
9.6
) we have

E(t)=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}-f(t).

Let
t\in[-\frac{T}{4},T]
and we write

\displaystyle E(t)

\displaystyle=\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)/5}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds+\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}-f(t)

\displaystyle=\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{T-s}ds-\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{T-s}ds

\displaystyle+\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)/5}p_{1,0}(s)\left(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\right)ds+\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}-f(t).

Then we estimate as follows,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{T-s}ds\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-s)\right|}\frac{1}{T-s}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-t)\right|}\frac{1}{T-t}\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t}ds\lesssim\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-t)\right|}

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)/5}p_{1,0}(s)\left(\frac{1}{t-s}-\frac{1}{T-s}\right)ds\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)/5}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-s)\right|}\frac{T-t}{(t-s)(T-s)}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)/5}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-s)\right|}\frac{T-t}{(T-s)^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-t)\right|}.

For the fourth term in
E
we have

\displaystyle\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle=p_{1,0}(t)\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{1}{t-s}ds-\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(t)-p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle=p_{1,0}(t)\left(\log(T-t)/5-2\log(\lambda_{*}(t))\right)-\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(t)-p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds.

Furthermore,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{t-(T-t)/5}^{t-\lambda_{*}(t)^{2}}\frac{p_{1,0}(t)-p_{1,0}(s)}{t-s}ds\right|\leq\sup_{-T\leq s\leq t}|\dot{p}_{1,0}(s)|(T-t)\leq\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-t)\right|}.

Since
p_{1,0}
satisfies the equation
\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{T-s}ds-p_{1,0}(t)\log(T-t)-\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}{\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}-f(t)=0
, we have

\displaystyle E(t)\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\left(\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{\left|\log(T-t)\right|}\right).

This is the desired estimate (
9.8
).
∎

Lemma 9.5
.

We have the estimate

\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds\leq C\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2(1-a)+\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{b}},\quad t\in[0,T]

for
a>1
and
b>0
.
For
\mu\in(0,1)
,
l\in\mathbb{R}
, we have

\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(T-s)^{\mu}}{(t-s)^{2}|\log(T-s)|^{l}}ds\leq C\frac{(T-t)^{\mu}}{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}|\log(T-t)|^{l}}.

Proof.

Let us write

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds

\displaystyle=\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds.

Then, for
t\in[0,T]
, we estimate,

\displaystyle\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds\leq C\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{1}{(T-s)^{a-\Theta}|\log(T-s)|^{b+2\Theta}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{1}{(T-t)^{a-\Theta-1}|\log(T-t)|^{b+2\Theta}}\leq C\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{(T-t)^{a-1}|\log(T-t)|^{b}}\leq C\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2(1-a)+\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{b}}

and

\displaystyle\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{b}}\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{1}{(t-s)^{a}}ds\leq C\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2a-2}|\log(T-t)|^{b}}.

The case for
t\in[-T,0]
is similar. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{a}|\log(T-s)|^{b}}ds\right|\leq\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{b}}\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda^{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{1}{(t-s)^{a}}ds\leq C\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta+2(1-a)}}{|\log(T-t)|^{b}}.

Similarly, we have

\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(T-s)^{\mu}}{(t-s)^{2}|\log(T-s)|^{l}}ds\leq C\frac{(T-t)^{\mu}}{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}|\log(T-t)|^{l}}.

∎

Lemma 9.6
.

Let
M=C|\log T|^{k-1}
,
k\in(0,1)
, then for
\|p_{1,1}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq M
, we have

\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](\cdot)\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}|\log T|^{k-1}.

Proof.

Observe that with the choice of
M
, if
\|p_{1,1}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq M
, then we have

\left|\frac{p_{1,1}}{p_{1,0}}\right|\leq\frac{C_{0}}{|\log T|^{k}}

since
T>0
is sufficiently small.
Let us recall that

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}[p_{1}]=\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{1}[p_{1}]+\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{2}[p_{1}]{\mathbf{c}}+\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{3}[p_{1}]{\mathbf{c}}-2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}},

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{1}[p_{1}]=-\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds-\int_{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds,

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{2}[p_{1}]=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\right)ds+\int_{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds,

\tilde{\mathcal{B}}_{3}[p_{1}]=-\frac{2}{3}\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}ds+\frac{2}{3}\mathbf{Z}.

From the definition, we have

\|2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}}\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}|\log T|^{k-1}.

For the other terms, we write

\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds,

\tilde{B}_{2,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)=\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+\frac{2}{3}\right)ds

and

\tilde{B}_{1,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)=\int_{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds,

\tilde{B}_{2,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)=\int_{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t}\frac{{\rm Re}[p_{0}(s)e^{-i\gamma(t)}]}{t-s}\Gamma_{5}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)ds.

From Lemma
9.5
and the asymptotic estimates for
\Gamma_{3}
,
\Gamma_{5}
, we have the following estimates,

\displaystyle|\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)|

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(p_{1,0}+p_{1,1})(s)}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}|\log(T-s)|}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|},

\displaystyle|\tilde{B}_{2,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t){\mathbf{c}}(t)|

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{0}(s)}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}}ds{\mathbf{c}}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma+\Theta}\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{1}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}|\log(T-s)|^{2}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}

and

\displaystyle|\tilde{B}_{1,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)|

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{1}{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}\left|(p_{1,0}+p_{1,1})(s)\right|ds\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|},

\displaystyle|\tilde{B}_{2,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t){\mathbf{c}}(t)|

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}}{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}\left|p_{0}(s)\right|ds(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}},

\displaystyle|\tilde{B}_{3}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t){\mathbf{c}}(t)|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|+C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}\left|\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{0}(s)-p_{0}(t)}{t-s}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

Combine all the above estimates, we obtain

\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](\cdot)\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}|\log T|^{k-1}.

∎

Lemma 9.7
.

Let
M=C|\log T|^{k-1}
, then for
\|p_{1,i}\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq M
,
i=1,2
, we have the following estimate,

\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](\cdot)-\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,2}](\cdot)\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{C}{|\log T|}\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

Proof.

Let us use the notations in Lemma
9.6
. The estimate for
2\lambda\dot{\mathbf{c}}
is from definition directly. For the other terms, we write

D_{1,a}:=\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)-\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,2}](t),\quad D_{2,a}:=\tilde{B}_{2,a}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)})(t)-\tilde{B}_{2,a}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)

D_{1,b}:=\tilde{B}_{1,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)-\tilde{B}_{1,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,2}](t),\quad D_{2,b}:=\tilde{B}_{2,b}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)})(t)-\tilde{B}_{2,b}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t),

D_{3}:=\tilde{B}_{3}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)})(t)-\tilde{B}_{3}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t).

Let us consider the term

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\zeta}\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,\zeta}](t)

\displaystyle=\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{|(p_{0}+p_{1})(t)|^{2}}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds

with
p_{1,\zeta}(t)=\zeta p_{1,1}(t)+(1-\zeta)p_{1,2}(t)
. Using the decaying estimates for
\Gamma_{3}
and Lemma
9.5
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{|(p_{0}+p_{1})(t)|^{2}}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{|p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)|}{t-s}\left(\frac{|(p_{0}+p_{\zeta})(t)|^{2}}{t-s}\right)^{\sigma}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}

\displaystyle=C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}.

Therefore we have

\displaystyle\left|D_{1,a}(t)\right|=\left|\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d}{d\zeta}\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,\zeta}](t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{|(p_{0}+p_{1})(t)|^{2}}{t-s}\right)-1\right)ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}.

Similarly, we estimate
D_{1,b}
as follows.

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\zeta}\tilde{B}_{1,b}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,\zeta}](t)

\displaystyle=\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{|(p_{0}+p_{1})(t)|^{2}}{t-s}\right)ds.

For this term we have

\displaystyle\left|D_{1,b}(t)\right|=\left|\int_{0}^{1}\frac{d}{d\zeta}\tilde{B}_{1,a}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,\zeta}](t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{|(p_{0}+p_{1})(t)|^{2}}{t-s}\right)ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{|(p_{0}+p_{\zeta})(t)|^{2}}\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}|p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s)|ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}.

Now we estimate
D_{2,a}
. We have

\displaystyle|D_{2,a}|=|\tilde{B}_{2,a}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)})(t)-\tilde{B}_{2,a}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)|

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma}\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{0}(s)}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}}ds({\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)}-{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma-1}\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{0}(s)}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}}ds\int_{t}^{T}(p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma-1}\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{0}(s)}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}}ds\int_{t}^{T}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma-1}\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{|\log T|}{(t-s)^{1+\sigma}|\log(T-s)|^{2}}ds\int_{t}^{T}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2\sigma-1}\frac{|\log T|(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-2\sigma}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}(T-t)

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}.

For
D_{2,b}
, we have

\displaystyle|D_{2,b}|=|\tilde{B}_{2,b}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)})(t)-\tilde{B}_{2,b}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)|

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-2}\left|\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}|p_{0}(s)|ds({\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)}-{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-2}\left|\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}|p_{0}(s)|ds\int_{t}^{T}(p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-2}\left|\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}|p_{0}(s)|ds\int_{t}^{T}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-2}\left|\frac{|\log T|(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}\int_{t}^{T}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{-2}\frac{|\log T|(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}(T-t)

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta+1}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}.

For
D_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle|D_{3}|=|\tilde{B}_{3}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)})(t)-\tilde{B}_{3}[p_{1}]({\mathbf{c}}^{(0)}+{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)|

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\left(|\dot{\lambda}_{*}|+\left|\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-\lambda^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{0}(s)-p_{0}(t)}{t-s}ds\right|\right)({\mathbf{c}}^{(1,1)}-{\mathbf{c}}^{(1,2)})(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\frac{|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}\left|\int_{t}^{T}(p_{1,1}(s)-p_{1,2}(s))ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\left|\frac{|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}\int_{t}^{T}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}(T-t)

\displaystyle\leq C\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta+1}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}}.

Combine all the above estimates, we obtain

\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](\cdot)-\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,2}](\cdot)\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{C}{|\log T|}\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

∎

Proof of Proposition
9.3
: Now we prove Proposition
9.3
based on the above estimates and the fixed point arguments. Indeed, we choose
k\in(0,1)
. Then from Lemma
9.1
we have

\|\mathcal{A}[p_{1,1}]\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq C\left(\|\eta\tilde{E}\|_{*,\Theta,k}+\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\right)

and

\|\eta\tilde{E}\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq C_{E}|\log T|^{k-1}

\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq C|\log T|^{k-1}.

Therefore we have

\|\mathcal{A}[p_{1,1}]\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq C\cdot C_{E}|\log T|^{k-1}+C|\log T|^{k-1}\leq C_{0}|\log T|^{k-1}

for fixing
C_{0}
large. Hence the operator maps
\overline{B}_{M}(0)
into itself for
M=C_{0}|\log T|^{k-1}
.
Moreover, we have

\|\mathcal{A}[p_{1,1}]-\mathcal{A}[p_{1,2}]\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}\leq C\|\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}]-\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,2}]\|_{*,\Theta,k}\leq\frac{C}{|\log T|}\|p_{1,1}-p_{1,2}\|_{*,\Theta,k+1}.

Then we obtain a fixed point of problem (
9.7
) by the contraction mapping theorem. ∎

9.2.
Estimates of the derivatives

Lemma 9.8
.

We have the following estimates for
p_{1,1}
,

(9.9)

\left|\dot{p}_{1,1}(t)\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}(T-t)},

(9.10)

\left|\ddot{p}_{1,1}(t)\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}(T-t)^{2}}.

Proof.

To prove (
9.9
), let us recall that
p_{1,1}
satisfies the following equation

\tilde{L}_{0}[p_{1,1}]+\eta(\frac{t}{T})\tilde{L}_{1}[p_{1,1}]+\eta(\frac{t}{T})E+\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)-\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3}{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}=0\text{ in }[-T,T].

Differentiate this equation with respect to
t
, we have

\displaystyle(1-\alpha_{1})|\log(T-t)|\dot{p}_{1,1}+(1-\alpha_{1})\frac{p_{1,1}}{T-t}

\displaystyle+\eta(\frac{t}{T})\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{1}[p_{1,1}]+\frac{1}{T}\eta^{\prime}\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)\tilde{L}_{1}[p_{1,1}]+\eta\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)\frac{d}{dt}E

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{T}\eta^{\prime}\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)E+\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)-\frac{d}{dt}\left(\frac{2\mathbf{Z}}{3}{\mathbf{c}}^{(1)}\right)=0.

Rewrite the above equation as

(1-\alpha_{1})|\log(T-t)|\dot{p}_{1,1}+\eta(\frac{t}{T})\tilde{L}_{1}[\dot{p}_{1,1}]+\mathcal{U}[\dot{p}_{1,1}](t)=h(t)

where
h
is a function satisfying

|h(t)|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}

and
\mathcal{U}
is the operator defined by

\mathcal{U}[\dot{p}_{1,1}]=-\int_{-T}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{\dot{p}_{1,1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{(\lambda(t))^{2}}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds-\int_{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{t}\frac{\dot{p}_{1,1}(s)}{t-s}\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{(\lambda(t))^{2}}{t-s}\right)ds.

Observe that One of the terms in
h
is
\eta(\frac{t}{T})\frac{d}{dt}E
, from the proof of Lemma
9.4
, we have

\left|\eta(\frac{t}{T})\frac{d}{dt}E\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}.

Let us recall the proof of Lemma
9.1
, we estimate the term
\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{10}[p_{1,1}]
as follows

\displaystyle\left|\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{10}[p_{1,0}]\right|=\left|\frac{d}{dt}\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{p_{1,0}(s)}{T-s}ds\right|=\left|\frac{p_{1,0}(t)}{T-t}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}.

Next we compute
\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{11}[p_{1,1}]
,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{11}[p_{1,1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int_{(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}^{T-t}\frac{p_{1,1}(t-r)}{r}dr

\displaystyle=\int_{(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}^{T-t}\frac{\dot{p}_{1,1}(t-r)}{r}dr-\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{T-t}

\displaystyle\quad+(1+\alpha_{1})\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{0}})}{T-t}

\displaystyle=\tilde{L}_{11}[\dot{p}_{1,1}]-\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{T-t}+(1+\alpha_{1})\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{0}})}{T-t}.

The last two terms can be estimated as follows

\left|\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{T-t}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\left|(1+\alpha_{1})\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{0}})}{T-t}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}.

Similarly, the term
\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{12}[p_{1,1}]
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{12}[p_{1,1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int_{0}^{T-t}\frac{p_{1,1}(t-r)}{T-t+r}dr

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T-t}\frac{\dot{p}_{1,1}(t-r)}{T-t+r}dr-\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{2(T-t)}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T-t}\frac{p_{1,1}(t-r)}{(T-t+r)^{2}}dr

\displaystyle=\tilde{L}_{12}[\dot{p}_{1,1}]-\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{2(T-t)}+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)}{(T-s)^{2}}ds

and

\left|\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{2(T-t)}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\left|\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)}{(T-s)^{2}}ds\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}.

The term
\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{13}[p_{1,1}]
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{13}[p_{1,1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int_{T-t}^{T+t}\frac{p_{1,1}(t-r)(T-t)}{r(T-t+r)}dr

\displaystyle=\int_{T-t}^{T+t}\frac{\dot{p}_{1,1}(t-r)(T-t)}{r(T-t+r)}dr+\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{2(T-t)}+\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T+t))(T-t)}{2T(T+t)}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{T-t}^{T+t}\frac{p_{1,1}(t-r)}{r(T-t+r)}dr+\int_{T-t}^{T+t}\frac{p_{1,1}(t-r)(T-t)}{r(T-t+r)^{2}}dr

\displaystyle=\tilde{L}_{13}[\dot{p}_{1,1}]+\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{2(T-t)}+\frac{p_{1,1}(-T)(T-t)}{2T(T+t)}

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)}{(t-s)(T-s)}ds+\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)(T-t)}{(t-s)(T-s)^{2}}ds

and

\left|\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t))}{2(T-t)}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\left|\frac{p_{1,1}(-T)(T-t)}{2T(T+t)}\right|\leq C\left|\frac{p_{1,1}(-T)}{2T}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)}{(t-s)(T-s)}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{(T-s)^{2}|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{p_{1,1}(s)(T-t)}{(t-s)(T-s)^{2}}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}(T-t)}{(T-s)^{3}|\log(T-s)|^{k+1}}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+1}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}.

The term
\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{14}[p_{1,1}]
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{L}_{14}[p_{1,1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\left((4\log(|\log(T-t)|)-2\log(|\log T|))p_{1,1}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{L}_{14}[\dot{p}_{1,1}]+\frac{4}{(T-t)|\log(T-t)|}p_{1,1}

and

\left|\frac{4}{(T-t)|\log(T-t)|}p_{1,1}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k+2}(T-t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}(T-t)}.

For the term
\frac{1}{T}\eta^{\prime}\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)\tilde{L}_{1}[p_{1,1}]
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{T}\eta^{\prime}\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)\tilde{L}_{1}[p_{1,1}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{T|\log(T-t)|^{k}}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{|\log T|^{k-1}(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|^{k}(T-t)}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}

since
|\log(T-t)|\sim|\log T|
in the interval
t\in[-\frac{T}{4},0]
where
\eta^{\prime}\left(\frac{t}{T}\right)
is not zero.
Now we consider the term
\frac{d}{dt}\tilde{B}[p_{1,0}+p_{1,1}](t)
. Observe that
one of these terms are

\frac{d}{dt}\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds,

we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)+1\right)ds=\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t+T}\frac{p_{1}(t-r)}{r}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{r}\right)+1\right)dr

\displaystyle=\int_{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t+T}\frac{\dot{p}_{1}(t-r)}{r}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{r}\right)+1\right)dr+\int_{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t+T}\frac{p_{1}(t-r)}{r}\left(\Gamma_{3}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{r}\right)\right)\frac{2\lambda(t)\lambda^{\prime}(t)}{r}dr

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{p_{1}(-T)}{t+T}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t+T}\right)+1\right)+\frac{p_{1}(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t))}{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\right)+1\right)2\lambda_{*}(t)\lambda_{*}^{\prime}(t)

and from Lemma
9.5
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}^{t+T}\frac{p_{1}(t-r)}{r}\left(\Gamma_{3}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{r}\right)\right)\frac{2\lambda(t)\lambda^{\prime}(t)}{r}dr\right|=\left|\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{p_{1}(s)}{t-s}\left(\Gamma_{3}^{\prime}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t-s}\right)\right)\frac{2\lambda(t)\lambda^{\prime}(t)}{t-s}ds\right|

\displaystyle\leq C|\lambda(t)\lambda^{\prime}(t)|\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{|p_{1}(s)|}{(t-s)^{2}}\left(\frac{t-s}{\lambda^{2}(t)}\right)^{\sigma}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\lambda(t)^{1-2\sigma}|\lambda^{\prime}(t)|\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\int_{-T}^{t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\frac{1}{(t-s)^{2-\sigma}}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(s))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|}ds

\displaystyle\leq C\lambda(t)^{1-2\sigma}|\lambda^{\prime}(t)|\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta+2(\sigma-1)}}{|\log(T-t)|}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{p_{1}(-T)}{t+T}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{t+T}\right)+1\right)\right|\leq\left|\frac{|p_{1}(-T)}{T}\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(2T))^{\Theta}}{|\log(2T)|T}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{p_{1}(t-\lambda_{*}^{2}(t))}{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\left(\Gamma_{3}\left(\frac{\lambda^{2}(t)}{\lambda_{*}^{2}(t)}\right)+1\right)2\lambda_{*}(t)\lambda_{*}^{\prime}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|}\frac{|\lambda_{*}^{\prime}(t)|}{\lambda_{*}(t)}\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t)|(T-t)}.

The other terms in
h
as well as the proof of (
9.10
) can be estimated similarly, we omit the details here.
∎

9.3.
Estimate of the remainder
R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]

Lemma 9.9
.

Let
p_{1,1}
be the solution constructed in Proposition
9.3
, then we have

|R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}(T-t)^{\alpha_{1}}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

Furthermore, it holds that

\left|\frac{d}{dt}R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}(T-t)^{\alpha_{1}-1}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

Proof.

From the definition
\mathcal{R}_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]=\mathcal{I}[p_{1,1}]-S_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]
and the estimates in Lemma
9.8
, we have

\displaystyle|R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{|p_{1,1}(t)-p_{1,1}(s)|}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{r\in(t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}},t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2})}|\dot{p}_{1,1}(r)|(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}(T-t)^{\alpha_{1}}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{d}{dt}\int_{t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}^{t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}\frac{p_{1,1}(t)-p_{1,1}(s)}{t-s}ds

\displaystyle=-\frac{d}{dt}\int_{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}\frac{p_{1,1}(t)-p_{1,1}(t-r)}{r}dr

\displaystyle=-\int_{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2}}^{(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{1}}}\frac{\dot{p}_{1,1}(t)-\dot{p}_{1,1}(t-r)}{r}dr-2(1+\alpha_{1})\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(\lambda_{*}(t))^{2})}{\lambda_{*}(t)}\dot{\lambda}_{*}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+\alpha_{1})\frac{p_{1,1}(t-(T-t)^{1+\alpha_{0}})}{T-t}.

From the estimates in Lemma
9.8
, we obtain

\left|\frac{d}{dt}R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}]\right|\leq C\|f\|_{*,\Theta,0}\frac{(\lambda_{*}(t))^{\Theta}(T-t)^{\alpha_{1}-1}}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

∎

Combine the results in Proposition
9.3
, Lemma
9.8
and Lemma
9.9
, define
\mathcal{R}_{{\mathbf{c}}}[f](t):=R_{\alpha_{1}}[p_{1,1}](t)
, then we obtain the conclusion of Proposition
8.2
.

Appendix A
Proof of technical lemmas

Several technical lemmas will be proved in this section.

A.1.
Decomposition of the linearization

Proof of Lemma
2.1
.

We compute

\displaystyle\Delta(\phi_{3}W)=

\displaystyle\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{3}-\left(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\sin^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{3}\right]W

\displaystyle+\left[\left(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}-\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{3}+2w_{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{3}\right]E_{1}+2\frac{\sin w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{3}}{\rho^{2}}E_{2},

\displaystyle\Delta(\phi_{1}E_{1})=

\displaystyle-\left[\left(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{1}+2w_{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{1}\right]W

\displaystyle+\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{1}-\left(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\cos^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{1}\right]E_{1}+2\frac{\cos w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{1}}{\rho^{2}}E_{2},

and

\displaystyle\Delta(\phi_{2}E_{2})=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\sin w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}W-\frac{2\cos w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}E_{1}

\displaystyle+\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{2}-\frac{\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right]E_{2}.

So we get

\displaystyle~~\Delta\phi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{3}-\left(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\sin^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{3}-\left(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{1}-2w_{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{2\sin w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right]W

\displaystyle~+\left[\left(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}-\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{3}+2w_{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{1}-\left(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\cos^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{1}-\frac{2\cos w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right]E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{2}-\frac{\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}+2\frac{\sin w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{3}}{\rho^{2}}+2\frac{\cos w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{1}}{\rho^{2}}\right]E_{2}.

Also, one has

\displaystyle\phi_{y_{1}}=

\displaystyle~\left(\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\right)(\phi_{3}W+\phi_{1}E_{1}+\phi_{2}E_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}-\cos\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{\sin\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]W

\displaystyle~+\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}+\cos\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{\sin\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}-\frac{\sin\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{3}-\frac{\sin\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{1}\right]E_{2}

\displaystyle\phi_{y_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\left(\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\right)(\phi_{3}W+\phi_{1}E_{1}+\phi_{2}E_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}-\sin\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{\cos\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]W

\displaystyle~+\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}+\sin\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{3}-\frac{\cos\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}+\frac{\cos\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{\cos\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{1}\right]E_{2}

and thus

\displaystyle U_{y_{1}}\wedge\phi_{y_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\cos\theta w_{\rho}\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}+\frac{\cos\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{1}\right]W

\displaystyle~+\frac{\sin\theta\sin w}{\rho}\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}-\frac{\cos\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]W+\frac{w_{\rho}\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{3}W

\displaystyle~-\frac{\sin\theta\sin w}{\rho}\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}-\sin\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{\cos\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{1}

\displaystyle~-\cos\theta w_{\rho}\left[\sin\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{\cos\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}-\sin\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{\cos\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{2},

\displaystyle\phi_{y_{1}}\wedge U_{y_{2}}=

\displaystyle~\frac{\cos\theta\sin w}{\rho}\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}+\frac{\sin\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]W

\displaystyle~-\sin\theta w_{\rho}\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}-\frac{\sin\theta\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{1}\right]W+\frac{w_{\rho}\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{3}W

\displaystyle~-\frac{\cos\theta\sin w}{\rho}\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}-\cos\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{\sin\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\sin\theta w_{\rho}\left[\cos\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}-\frac{\sin\theta}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}-\cos\theta w_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{\sin\theta\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{2}.

Therefore, linearization

\Delta_{y}\phi-2U_{y_{1}}\wedge\phi_{y_{2}}-2\phi_{y_{1}}\wedge U_{y_{2}}=0

implies

\displaystyle~\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{3}-\left(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\sin^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{3}-\left(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}+\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{1}-2w_{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{1}-\frac{2\sin w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right]W

\displaystyle~+\left[\left(w_{\rho\rho}+\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}-\frac{\sin w\cos w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{3}+2w_{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{1}-\left(w_{\rho}^{2}+\frac{\cos^{2}w}{\rho^{2}}\right)\phi_{1}-\frac{2\cos w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}\right]E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left[\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi_{2}+\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\partial_{\theta\theta}\phi_{2}-\frac{\phi_{2}}{\rho^{2}}+2\frac{\sin w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{3}}{\rho^{2}}+2\frac{\cos w\partial_{\theta}\phi_{1}}{\rho^{2}}\right]E_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~2\left[\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}+\frac{w_{\rho}\cos w}{\rho}\phi_{1}+\frac{\sin w}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}+2\frac{w_{\rho}\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{3}\right]W

\displaystyle~+2\left[-\frac{\sin w}{\rho}{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{3}+\frac{\sin w}{\rho}w_{\rho}\phi_{1}\right]E_{1}+2\left[-\frac{w_{\rho}}{\rho}{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{3}+\frac{w_{\rho}\sin w}{\rho}\phi_{2}\right]E_{2},

and the proof is complete by collecting terms in the same direction.

∎

Proof of Lemma
2.2
.

First, we notice the fact

U_{r}=\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)Q_{\gamma}E_{1},\quad\frac{1}{r}\partial_{\theta}U=-\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}.

By direct computation, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}U_{r}\wedge\Phi_{\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{r}\left(\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}\right)\wedge\Phi_{\theta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda r}w_{\rho}(\rho)(Q_{\gamma}E_{1}\wedge\Phi_{\theta})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\lambda r}w_{\rho}(\rho)\left[(-\Phi_{\theta}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}W))Q_{\gamma}E_{2}+(\Phi_{\theta}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}E_{2}))Q_{\gamma}W\right]

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\Phi_{r}\wedge U_{\theta}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{r}\wedge\left(-\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(-\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)\right)\Phi_{r}\wedge(Q_{\gamma}E_{2})

\displaystyle=\left(-\frac{1}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)\right)\left[\left(-\Phi_{r}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}W)\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+(\Phi_{r}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}E_{1}))Q_{\gamma}W\right].

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi]=

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{\lambda r}w_{\rho}(\rho)\left[(-\Phi_{\theta}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}W))Q_{\gamma}E_{2}+(\Phi_{\theta}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}E_{2}))Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\frac{2}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)\left[\left(-\Phi_{r}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}W)\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+(\Phi_{r}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}E_{1}))Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{\lambda}w_{\rho}(\rho)\left(\Phi_{r}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}W)\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+\frac{2}{\lambda r}w_{\rho}(\rho)(\Phi_{\theta}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}W))Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\frac{2}{\lambda r}w_{\rho}(\rho)\left[r(\Phi_{r}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}E_{1}))-(\Phi_{\theta}\cdot(Q_{\gamma}E_{2}))\right]Q_{\gamma}W.

∎

Proof of Lemma
2.3
.

Clearly,

\Phi_{r}=\cos\theta\partial_{x_{1}}\Phi+\sin\theta\partial_{x_{2}}\Phi,\quad\frac{1}{r}\Phi_{\theta}=-\sin\theta\partial_{x_{1}}\Phi+\cos\theta\partial_{x_{2}}\Phi.

We have
(\Phi\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{j})=(Q_{-\gamma}\Phi\cdot E_{j})
and write
\phi=Q_{-\gamma}\Phi=(\phi_{1},~\phi_{2},~\phi_{3})^{T}
so that

(A.1)

\Phi=\begin{bmatrix}\varphi_{1}+i\varphi_{2}\\
\varphi_{3}\\
\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}e^{i\gamma}(\phi_{1}+i\phi_{2})\\
\phi_{3}\\
\end{bmatrix}.

Then, one has

\displaystyle\phi_{r}\cdot W

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sin w\Big[[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\cos(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]\Big]

\displaystyle+\cos w[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{3}\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{3}\sin\theta]

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\phi_{\theta}\cdot W

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sin w\Big[[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]+\cos(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}]+\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}-\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}]\Big]

\displaystyle+\cos w[-\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{3}\sin\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{3}\cos\theta]

and

\displaystyle\phi_{r}\cdot E_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\cos w\Big[[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\cos(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]\Big]

\displaystyle-\sin w[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{3}\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{3}\sin\theta]

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\phi_{\theta}\cdot E_{2}

\displaystyle=[-\sin\theta\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+\cos\theta\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}](-\sin\theta)+[-\sin\theta\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}+\cos\theta\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}](\cos\theta)
