\displaystyle=[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}\sin^{2}\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}\cos^{2}\theta]-\frac{1}{2}\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]

\displaystyle=[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}\frac{1-\cos(2\theta)}{2}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}\frac{\cos(2\theta)+1}{2}]-\frac{1}{2}\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]-\frac{1}{2}\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]-\frac{1}{2}\cos(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]

\displaystyle\phi_{r}\cdot E_{1}-\frac{1}{r}\phi_{\theta}\cdot E_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\cos w\Big[[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\cos(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]\Big]

\displaystyle-\sin w[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{3}\cos\theta+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{3}\sin\theta]

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}]+\frac{1}{2}\sin(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{1}+\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{2}]+\frac{1}{2}\cos(2\theta)[\partial_{x_{1}}\phi_{1}-\partial_{x_{2}}\phi_{2}].

Combining above terms with above Lemma and (
A.1
), we obtain the desired decomposition.
∎

A.2.
Analysis of the new error

Proof of Lemma
4.1
.

We have

\displaystyle~S[U_{*}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\mathcal{R}_{U}+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}-{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{(0)}+\Delta\Phi^{(0)})+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}-{\partial}_{t}\Phi^{(1)}+\Delta\Phi^{(1)})+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle~+(1-\eta_{1})\Big(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(\pm 2)}+\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 2)}\Big)+(\Delta\eta_{1}-{\partial}_{t}\eta_{1})\Phi_{*}+2\nabla\Phi_{*}\cdot\nabla\eta_{1}

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\left[\phi_{1}^{(2)}{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}E_{1})+\phi_{2}^{(2)}{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}E_{2})+2\theta_{t}(\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-\phi_{2}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1})+{\partial}_{\rho}\Phi_{U^{\perp}}^{(2)}\rho_{t}\right]

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\left[\phi_{1}^{(-2)}{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}E_{1})+\phi_{2}^{(-2)}{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}E_{2})-2\theta_{t}(\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-\phi_{2}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1})+{\partial}_{\rho}\Phi_{U^{\perp}}^{(-2)}\rho_{t}\right]

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\left[\sin 2\theta\psi_{2}{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}W)+2\cos 2\theta\psi_{2}\theta_{t}Q_{\gamma}W+\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}\rho_{t}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})

\displaystyle~-2{\partial}_{x_{2}}\eta_{1}{\partial}_{x_{1}}(Q_{\gamma}W)\wedge\Phi_{*}-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\eta_{1}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(Q_{\gamma}W)-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\mathcal{R}_{U^{\perp}}+\mathcal{R}_{U}+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{0})+\eta_{1}(\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\tilde{\mathcal{R}}^{1})+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle~+(1-\eta_{1})\Big(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(\pm 2)}+\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 2)}\Big)+(\Delta\eta_{1}-{\partial}_{t}\eta_{1})\Phi_{*}+2\nabla\Phi_{*}\cdot\nabla\eta_{1}

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\sin w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)](\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~-2\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)\cos 2\theta\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\cos w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)](\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+2\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)[\phi_{2}^{(2)}-\phi_{2}^{(-2)}]Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)\sin 2\theta\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\cos w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)](\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+2\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)[\phi_{1}^{(-2)}-\phi_{1}^{(2)}]Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~-\eta_{1}\sin w[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)]\sin 2\theta\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\eta_{1}\tilde{L}_{U}[\Phi^{(0)}+\Phi^{(1)}]-\frac{2}{r}{\partial}_{r}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})

\displaystyle~-\frac{2}{r}\Big[{\partial}_{r}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})+{\partial}_{r}(\eta_{1}\Phi_{*})\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})\Big]

\displaystyle~-2{\partial}_{x_{2}}\eta_{1}{\partial}_{x_{1}}(Q_{\gamma}W)\wedge\Phi_{*}-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\eta_{1}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(Q_{\gamma}W),

where we have used

\displaystyle~\rho_{t}=-\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho),

\displaystyle~\theta_{t}=\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta),

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}W)=-\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)w_{\rho}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)]\sin wQ_{\gamma}E_{2},

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}E_{1})=\lambda^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\cos\theta+\dot{\xi}_{2}\sin\theta+\dot{\lambda}\rho)w_{\rho}Q_{\gamma}W+[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)]\cos wQ_{\gamma}E_{2},

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{t}(Q_{\gamma}E_{2})=-[\dot{\gamma}+\lambda^{-1}\rho^{-1}(\dot{\xi}_{1}\sin\theta-\dot{\xi}_{2}\cos\theta)](\cos wQ_{\gamma}E_{1}+\sin wQ_{\gamma}W).

We collect some extra terms produced by the cut-off
\eta_{1}
and define

(A.2)

\displaystyle E_{\eta_{1}}:=

\displaystyle(1-\eta_{1})\Big(\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(0)}+\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 1)}+\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(\pm 2)}+\mathcal{E}_{U}^{(\pm 2)}\Big)+(\Delta\eta_{1}-{\partial}_{t}\eta_{1})\Phi_{*}+2\nabla\Phi_{*}\cdot\nabla\eta_{1}

\displaystyle-2{\partial}_{x_{2}}\eta_{1}{\partial}_{x_{1}}(Q_{\gamma}W)\wedge\Phi_{*}-2{\partial}_{x_{1}}\eta_{1}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{x_{2}}(Q_{\gamma}W)-\frac{2\eta_{1}{\partial}_{r}\eta_{1}}{r}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}\Phi_{*}

\displaystyle-\frac{2{\partial}_{r}\eta_{1}}{r}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2}).

To further expand the error, we use (
4.17
) and first compute

\displaystyle{\partial}_{r}\Phi^{(0)}=\begin{bmatrix}e^{i\theta}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\\
0\\
\end{bmatrix},\quad{\partial}_{\theta}\Phi^{(0)}=\begin{bmatrix}ire^{i\theta}\psi^{0}\\
0\\
\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle{\partial}_{r}\Phi^{(1)}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\\
\end{bmatrix},\quad{\partial}_{\theta}\Phi^{(1)}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\\
\end{bmatrix},

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{r}(\Phi^{(2)}_{U^{\perp}}+\Phi^{(-2)}_{U^{\perp}}+\Phi^{(2)}_{U})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\lambda^{-1}\left[{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-w_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left[{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-w_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\sin 2\theta({\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}W+w_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}),

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{\theta}(\Phi^{(2)}_{U^{\perp}}+\Phi^{(-2)}_{U^{\perp}}+\Phi^{(2)}_{U})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~-\phi_{2}^{(2)}\sin wQ_{\gamma}W+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}-\cos w\phi_{2}^{(2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\cos w\phi_{1}^{(2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~-\phi_{2}^{(-2)}\sin wQ_{\gamma}W+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}-\cos w\phi_{2}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)}+\cos w\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\psi_{2}(2\cos 2\theta Q_{\gamma}W+\sin 2\theta\sin wQ_{\gamma}E_{2}).

We then have

\displaystyle{\partial}_{r}\Phi_{*}=

\displaystyle~\begin{bmatrix}e^{i\theta}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\\
0\\
\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\\
\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left[{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-w_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left[{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-w_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\sin 2\theta({\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}W+w_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}E_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos wQ_{\gamma}W-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin wQ_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left[{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-w_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left[{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}E_{2}-w_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}Q_{\gamma}W\right]

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\sin 2\theta({\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}W+w_{\rho}\psi_{2}Q_{\gamma}E_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left[\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}-w_{\rho}(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})\right]Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{2},

\displaystyle{\partial}_{\theta}\Phi_{*}=

\displaystyle~\begin{bmatrix}ire^{i\theta}\psi^{0}\\
0\\
\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\\
\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle~-\phi_{2}^{(2)}\sin wQ_{\gamma}W+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}-\cos w\phi_{2}^{(2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\cos w\phi_{1}^{(2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~-\phi_{2}^{(-2)}\sin wQ_{\gamma}W+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}-\cos w\phi_{2}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)}+\cos w\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\psi_{2}(2\cos 2\theta Q_{\gamma}W+\sin 2\theta\sin wQ_{\gamma}E_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+{\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]Q_{\gamma}E_{2}+\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos wQ_{\gamma}W-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin wQ_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~-\phi_{2}^{(2)}\sin wQ_{\gamma}W+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}-\cos w\phi_{2}^{(2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\cos w\phi_{1}^{(2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~-\phi_{2}^{(-2)}\sin wQ_{\gamma}W+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}-\cos w\phi_{2}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{1}+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)}+\cos w\phi_{1}^{(-2)})Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\psi_{2}(2\cos 2\theta Q_{\gamma}W+\sin 2\theta\sin wQ_{\gamma}E_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}.

So we obtain

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{r}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}\Phi_{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~-\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~+\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~-\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\Bigg[\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left[\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}-w_{\rho}(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})\right]\Bigg]

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~-\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle~\quad\times\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)Q_{\gamma}E_{2},

and

\displaystyle~{\partial}_{r}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}\Phi_{*}+{\partial}_{r}\Phi_{*}\wedge{\partial}_{\theta}(c_{1}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}Q_{\gamma}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~-\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+\cos w(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})+\sin 2\theta\sin w\psi_{2}\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]-{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\sin w+({\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\theta}\phi_{1}^{(-2)})-\cos w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\cos w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~-\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}ir\psi^{0}\right]+{\rm Im}(re^{-i\theta}\psi^{1})\cos w-\sin w(\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(-2)})+2\psi_{2}\cos 2\theta\right)

\displaystyle~\quad\times\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}\sin w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

\displaystyle~+\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]\times\sin^{2}w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}W

\displaystyle~-\Bigg[\left(\sin w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\cos w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left[\sin 2\theta{\partial}_{\rho}\psi_{2}-w_{\rho}(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{1}^{(-2)})\right]\Bigg]\times\sin^{2}w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~+\left({\rm Im}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]+\lambda^{-1}({\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{2}^{(-2)})\right)\times\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}E_{1}

\displaystyle~-\Bigg[\left(\cos w{\rm Re}\left[e^{-i\gamma}(\psi^{0}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0})\right]-{\rm Re}[e^{-i\theta}(\psi^{1}+\frac{r^{2}}{z}{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1})]\sin w\right)

\displaystyle~\qquad+\lambda^{-1}\left(\sin 2\theta w_{\rho}\psi_{2}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(2)}+{\partial}_{\rho}\phi_{1}^{(-2)}\right)\Bigg]\times\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta)Q_{\gamma}E_{2}

since

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{r}(c_{1}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})=\lambda^{-1}w_{\rho}(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)(\cos wW+\sin wE_{1}),

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{\theta}(c_{1}\mathcal{Z}_{1,1}+c_{2}\mathcal{Z}_{1,2})=\sin w(c_{2}\cos\theta-c_{1}\sin\theta)W+\sin^{2}w(c_{1}\cos\theta+c_{2}\sin\theta)E_{2}.

Collecting all the identities above, we conclude the desired results.
∎

To estimate terms appearing in the remainder
\mathcal{R}_{*}
, we give now several estimates for the corrections
\psi^{0}
,
\psi^{1}
,
\phi_{j}^{(k)}
,
j=1,2
,
k=\pm 2
, and
\psi_{2}
, where their definitions can be found in (
4.6
), (
4.9
), (
4.10
), (
4.11
), (
4.12
) and (
4.13
).

By the definition of
k(z,t)
in (
4.6
), we have

|\psi^{j}|\lesssim\int_{-T}^{t}\frac{|p_{j}(s)|}{t-s}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta\leq 1\}}+\zeta^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{\zeta>1\}}\right)\Big|_{\zeta=z^{2}(t-s)^{-1}}ds,\quad j=0,~1,

where
\mathbf{1}_{A}
denotes the characteristic function of the set
A
. For instance, we estimate

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}|\lesssim z^{-2}\int_{-T}^{t}|p_{1}(s)|ds\lesssim z^{-2}(t+T)T^{\Theta}|\log T|^{-1-2\Theta}~\mbox{ for }~z^{2}\geq t,

where we have used

|p_{1}|\sim\frac{\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|},\quad\lambda_{*}=\frac{(T-t)|\log T|}{|\log(T-t)|^{2}}.

For
z^{2}<t
, we have

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}|\lesssim\int_{-T}^{t-z^{2}}\frac{|p_{1}(s)|}{t-s}ds+z^{-2}\int_{t-z^{2}}^{t}|p_{1}(s)|ds,

and if
t\leq\frac{T}{2}
,

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}|\lesssim T^{\Theta}|\log T|^{-1-2\Theta}\left\langle\log\Big(\frac{t+T}{z^{2}}\Big)\right\rangle.

If
z^{2}<t
,
z^{2}<T-t
and
t>\frac{T}{2}
, one has

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle~\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{|p_{1}(s)|}{T-s}ds+\int_{t-(T-t)}^{t-z^{2}}\frac{|p_{1}(s)|}{t-s}ds+z^{-2}\int_{t-z^{2}}^{t}|p_{1}(s)|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~|\log T|^{\Theta}\int_{-T}^{t-(T-t)}\frac{(T-s)^{\Theta-1}}{|\log(T-s)|^{1+2\Theta}}ds+|p_{1}(t)|\log\frac{T-t}{z^{2}}+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta}(t)|\log(T-t)|^{-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\frac{T^{\Theta}}{|\log T|^{1+\Theta}}.

If
z^{2}<t
,
z^{2}\geq T-t
and
t>\frac{T}{2}
, we estimate

\displaystyle|\psi^{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle~\frac{T^{\Theta}}{|\log T|^{1+\Theta}}+z^{-2}\int_{t-z^{2}}^{t}\frac{(T-s)^{\Theta}|\log T|^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-s)|^{1+2\Theta}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\frac{T^{\Theta}}{|\log T|^{1+\Theta}}+\frac{(T-t+z^{2})^{\Theta}}{|\log(T-t+z^{2})|^{1+2\Theta}}.

Above estimates then give

|\psi^{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{z^{2}<t\}}+z^{-2}(t+T)T^{\Theta}|\log T|^{-1-2\Theta}\mathbf{1}_{\{z^{2}\geq t\}},

and similarly,

(A.3)

|\psi^{1}|+z|{\partial}_{z}\psi^{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{z^{2}<t\}}+z^{-2}(t+T)T^{\Theta}|\log T|^{-1-2\Theta}\mathbf{1}_{\{z^{2}\geq t\}}.

\psi^{0}
is estimated in the same way. One directly checks that

(A.4)

|\psi^{0}|+z|{\partial}_{z}\psi^{0}|\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{\{z^{2}<t\}}+z^{-2}(t+T)|\log T|^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{\{z^{2}\geq t\}}.

Terms
\phi_{j}^{(k)}
,
j=1,2
,
k=\pm 2
and
\psi_{2}
are estimated via the linear theory in both
W
-direction and on
W^{\perp}
in mode
\pm 2
, namely Proposition
7.2
and Proposition
7.7
with
k=\pm 2
.

In the scalar equation (
4.11
), the right hand sides for modes
j=2
and
j=-2
satisfy

\displaystyle~\left|\lambda^{2}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(2)}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}\right|=\Big|-ic_{1}c_{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin we^{2i\theta}(1-\cos w)\Big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(t)\rho\langle\rho\rangle^{-8},

\displaystyle~\left|\lambda^{2}\left(Q_{-\gamma}\mathcal{E}_{U^{\perp}}^{(-2)}\right)_{\mathbb{C}}\right|=\Big|-ic_{1}c_{2}w^{2}_{\rho}\sin we^{-2i\theta}(1+\cos w)\Big|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(t)\rho^{3}\langle\rho\rangle^{-8},

where we have used (
6.16
). Then Proposition
7.2
implies

(A.5)

\displaystyle|\varphi_{2}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(t)\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta},

\displaystyle|\varphi_{-2}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(t)\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}

for some
\delta\in(0,1)
and close to
1
. In fact, a more careful inspection on the proof of Proposition
7.2
enables one to obtain faster spatial decay for corrections in higher modes as well as vanishing properties near the origin. For instance,
\delta
can be taken close to
3
for mode
2
and close to
1
for mode
-2
. Similarly, in (
4.13
), since the RHS

|2c_{1}c_{2}\rho w_{\rho}^{2}\sin^{2}w|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(t)\rho^{3}\langle\rho\rangle^{-8},

one has

(A.6)

|\psi_{2}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta}(t)\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta_{1}}

by Proposition
7.7
, where
\delta_{1}
can be taken close to
2
.

One then uses the bounds (
A.3
), (
A.4
), (
A.5
) and (
A.6
) and directly checks that

(A.7)

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{R}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}W\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg[1+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta-2}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-2\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-4-\delta}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\}},

\displaystyle\Big|\mathcal{R}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{1}\Big|,~\Big|\mathcal{R}_{*}\cdot Q_{\gamma}E_{2}\Big|\lesssim

\displaystyle\Bigg[1+\lambda_{*}^{2\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-\delta}+\lambda_{*}^{4\Theta-2}\bigg(\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-2\delta}+\langle\rho\rangle^{-2-\delta-\delta_{1}}\bigg)+\lambda_{*}^{5\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-1-\delta}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{*}^{\Theta-1}\langle\rho\rangle^{-2}+\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-2}\langle\rho\rangle^{-3-\delta}\Bigg]\mathbf{1}_{\{\rho\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{-1}\}},

where the expression of the remainder
\mathcal{R}_{*}
is given in Lemma
4.1
, and we have used

|{\mathbf{c}}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{\Theta},\quad|\dot{\xi}|\lesssim\lambda_{*}^{3\Theta-1}.

For the remaining terms
E_{\eta_{1}}
(defined in (
A.2
)) supported outside of the inner region, we have

(A.8)

|E_{\eta_{1}}|\lesssim 1

by (
A.3
), (
A.4
), (
A.5
), (
A.6
), and (
4.4
).

Appendix B
Spectral analysis of the linearized Liouville equation

B.1.
Generalized eigenfunctions and density of spectral measure

We consider the operator

L_{0}:=\partial_{rr}+\frac{1}{r}\partial_{r}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}},

which has kernels

K_{1}=\frac{r^{2}-1}{r^{2}+1},\quad K_{2}=\frac{(r^{2}-1)\log r-2}{r^{2}+1}.

The operator
L_{0}
corresponds to the linearization at mode
0
of the Liouville equation. Let
u(\rho)=r^{-1/2}v(r)
. Then

L_{0}(u)=r^{-1/2}\mathcal{L}_{0}v,

with

\mathcal{L}_{0}v:={\partial}_{rr}v+\frac{v}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8v}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}.

The new operator
\mathcal{L}_{0}
has kernel

\Phi_{0}^{0}(r)=\frac{r^{1/2}(r^{2}-1)}{r^{2}+1},

and the other one is given by

\displaystyle\Theta_{0}^{0}(r)=

\displaystyle-\Phi_{0}^{0}(r)\int\frac{1}{(\Phi(r))^{2}}dr

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{r^{1/2}(r^{2}-1)}{r^{2}+1}\left(\log r-\frac{2}{r^{2}-1}+C\right)

for which

W[\Theta_{0}^{0},\Phi_{0}^{0}]=1.

Here for any
C
,
\Theta_{0}^{0}(1)=0
, and we take
C=0
.

Lemma B.1
.

(
[
37
, Lemma 4.2]
,
[
23
]
)
The spectrum of
\mathcal{L}_{0}
equals

spec(-\mathcal{L}_{0})=\{\xi_{d}\}\cup[0,\infty),

where the only negative eigenvalue

\xi_{d}
is negative and simple, and its corresponding eigenfunction
\phi_{d}
has exponential decay.

Remark B.1.1
.

The operator
\mathcal{L}_{0}
has a resonance at zero since
\mathcal{L}_{0}[\Phi_{0}^{0}]=0
and
\Theta_{0}^{0}\not\in L^{2}(dr)
.

We now consider the fundamental system of solutions
\phi(r,z)
and
\theta(r,z)
to

-\mathcal{L}_{0}y=zy

for all
z\in\mathbb{C}
so that

W[\theta(\cdot,z),\phi(\cdot,z)]=1.

Notice that these functions are entire in
z
,
\phi(r,0)=c\Phi_{0}^{0}(r)
for some normalization constant
c
. Let
\psi(r,z)
be a Weyl-Titchmarsh solution. The generalized Weyl-Titchmarsh
m
function is defined as

C\psi(\cdot,z)=\theta(\cdot,z)+m(z)\phi(\cdot,z)

for some constant
C\neq 0
. Then

m(z)=\frac{W[\theta(\cdot,z),\psi(\cdot,z)]}{W[\psi(\cdot,z),\phi(\cdot,z)]}.

A spectral measure of
\mathcal{L}_{0}
is obtained as

\rho((\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}])=\frac{1}{\pi}\lim_{\delta\to 0^{+}}\lim_{\epsilon\to 0^{+}}\int_{\lambda_{1}+\delta}^{\lambda_{2}+\delta}{\rm Im}~m(\lambda+i\epsilon)d\lambda.

For the detailed definitions and properties, see
[
23
]
.

Proposition B.2
.

The
m
function is

d\rho=\delta_{\xi_{d}}+\rho(\xi)d\xi,\quad\rho(\xi)=\pi^{-1}\mathrm{Im}\ m(\xi+i0^{+}).

The distorted Fourier transform (DFT) defined as

\mathcal{F}:f\rightarrow\hat{f},

\hat{f}(\xi_{d})=\int_{0}^{\infty}\phi_{d}(r)f(r)dr,\quad\hat{f}(\xi)=\lim\limits_{b\rightarrow\infty}\int_{0}^{b}\phi(r,\xi)f(r)dr,\quad\xi\geq 0,

is a unitary operator from
L^{2}({{\mathbb{R}}}^{+})
to
L^{2}(\{\xi_{d}\}\cup{{\mathbb{R}}}^{+},\rho)
, and its inverse is given by

\mathcal{F}^{-1}:\hat{f}\rightarrow f(r)=\hat{f}(\xi_{d})\phi_{d}(r)+\lim\limits_{\mu\rightarrow\infty}\int_{0}^{\mu}\phi(r,\xi)\hat{f}(\xi)\rho(\xi)d\xi.

Next, we want to give the asymptotic expansion of distorted basis
\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)
satisfying
-\mathcal{L}_{0}\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)=\xi\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)
.

Proposition B.3
.

For any
\xi\in\mathbb{C}
,
\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)
admits
the asymptotic expansion

\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)=\Phi_{0}^{0}(r)+r^{1/2}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{\infty}(-r^{2}\xi)^{j}\Phi_{j}^{0}(r^{2}),

which converges absolutely for any fixed
r
,
\xi
and converges uniformly if
r^{2}\xi
remains bounded with a fixed
r
. Here for
j\geq 1
,
\Phi_{j}^{0}(u)
are smooth functions in
u\geq 0
satisfying

\displaystyle\Phi_{1}^{0}(u)=

\displaystyle\frac{u-1}{12u(u+1)^{2}}\Bigg[(3u-2\pi^{2})(1+u)+6(1+u)\log(1+u)[2+\log(1+u)-2\log u]

\displaystyle+12(1+u){\rm Polylog}\left(2,\frac{1}{1+u}\right)\Bigg]+\frac{(u+3)\log(u+1)-3u}{u(u+1)},

|\Phi_{j}^{0}(u)|\leq C,\quad j\geq 2

for a large constant
C>0
.

Proof.

We look for

\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)=r^{1/2}\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\infty}(-\xi)^{j}f_{j}(r),\quad f_{0}(r)=\frac{r^{2}-1}{r^{2}+1}

with

\mathcal{L}_{0}(r^{1/2}f_{j})=r^{1/2}f_{j-1},\quad f_{-1}\equiv 0.

Then above ansatz yields the recurrence relation

\displaystyle f_{j}(r)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{r}r^{-1/2}\left[\Phi_{0}^{0}(r)\Theta_{0}^{0}(s)-\Theta_{0}^{0}(r)\Phi_{0}^{0}(s)\right]s^{1/2}f_{j-1}(s)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{r}\left[\frac{(r^{2}-1)}{r^{2}+1}\frac{s\left[2-(s^{2}-1)\log s\right]}{s^{2}+1}+\frac{\left[(r^{2}-1)\log r-2\right]}{r^{2}+1}\frac{s(s^{2}-1)}{s^{2}+1}\right]f_{j-1}(s)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{r}\frac{2s(r^{2}-s^{2})+s(s^{2}-1)(r^{2}-1)\log(r/s)}{(r^{2}+1)(s^{2}+1)}f_{j-1}(s)ds

Now we change variable
v=s^{2}
,
u=r^{2}
and define

\tilde{f}_{j}(v)=f_{j}(s).

In particular,

\tilde{f}_{0}(u)=\frac{u-1}{u+1}.

Then we get

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{j}(u)=\int_{0}^{u}\frac{4(u-v)+(v-1)(u-1)\log(u/v)}{4(u+1)(v+1)}\tilde{f}_{j-1}(v)dv.

We first consider
\tilde{f}_{1}(u)

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{1}(u)=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{u}\frac{4(u-z)(z-1)-(z-1)^{2}(u-1)\log(z/u)}{4(u+1)(z+1)^{2}}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1+u}\int_{0}^{u}\frac{(u-z)(z-1)}{(z+1)^{2}}dz-\frac{u-1}{4(u+1)}\int_{0}^{u}\frac{(z-1)^{2}\log(z/u)}{(z+1)^{2}}dz,

where we have

\int_{0}^{u}\frac{(u-z)(z-1)}{(z+1)^{2}}dz=(u+3)\log(u+1)-3u,

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{u}\frac{(z-1)^{2}\log(z/u)}{(z+1)^{2}}dz

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{3(1+u)}\Bigg[3u(1+u)-2\pi^{2}(1+u)-15u\log u-3u^{2}\log u+12(1+u)\log(1+u)

\displaystyle+6(1+u)\log^{2}(1+u)+3[u(5+u)-4(1+u)\log(1+u)](\log u)

\displaystyle+12(1+u){\rm Polylog}\left(2,\frac{1}{1+u}\right)\Bigg]

with

{\rm Polylog}\left(2,\frac{1}{1+u}\right)\sim\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}+(\log u-1)u-\frac{1}{4}(2\log u+1)u^{2}+\frac{1}{18}(6\log u+7)u^{3}+O(u^{4}\log u)~\mbox{ for }~u\ll 1,\\
&\frac{1}{u}+\frac{1}{4u^{2}}+\frac{1}{9u^{3}}+O\left(\frac{1}{u^{4}}\right)~\mbox{ for }~u\gg 1,\\
\end{aligned}\right.

and for
u\ll 1

\displaystyle\frac{u-1}{u+1}{\rm Polylog}\left(2,\frac{1}{1+u}\right)=

\displaystyle~-\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}+\frac{1}{3}(3+\pi^{2}-3\log u)u+\frac{1}{12}(-21-4\pi^{2}+30\log u)u^{2}

\displaystyle~+\frac{1}{9}(10+3\pi^{2}-30\log u)u^{3}+O(u^{4}\log u).

So we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{1}(u)=

\displaystyle\frac{(u+3)\log(u+1)-3u}{u+1}

\displaystyle+\frac{u-1}{12(u+1)^{2}}\Bigg[(3u-2\pi^{2})(1+u)+6(1+u)\log(1+u)[2+\log(1+u)-2\log u]

\displaystyle+12(1+u){\rm Polylog}\left(2,\frac{1}{1+u}\right)\Bigg],

and

(B.1)

\tilde{f}_{1}(u)\sim\left\{\begin{aligned} &-\frac{u}{4}+\frac{u^{2}}{4}-\frac{2u^{3}}{9}+O(u^{4})~&\mbox{ for }~u\ll 1,\\
&\frac{u}{4}-\frac{3\log^{2}u-12\log u+21+\pi^{2}}{6}+\frac{6\log^{2}u+39+2\pi^{2}}{6u}+O\left(\frac{\log^{2}u}{u^{2}}\right)~&\mbox{ for }~u\gg 1.\\
\end{aligned}\right.

Next
we estimate
\tilde{f}_{j}(u)
inductively. We consider

\tilde{f}_{j}(u)=\int_{0}^{u}\frac{4(u-v)+(v-1)(u-1)\log(u/v)}{4(u+1)(v+1)}\tilde{f}_{j-1}(v)dv

and bound the kernel function as

\left|\frac{4(u-v)+(v-1)(u-1)\log(u/v)}{4(u+1)(v+1)}\right|\leq C[\log(u/v)+1]

for some constant
C>0
since
v\leq u
. Then from (
B.1
) we have

|\tilde{f}_{1}(u)|\leq Cu,

and thus

\displaystyle|\tilde{f}_{2}(u)|\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{u}[\log(u/v)+1]|\tilde{f}_{1}(v)|dv

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{u}v[\log(u/v)+1]dv

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cu^{2}.

By induction, clearly one has

|\tilde{f}_{j}(u)|\leq Cu^{j}.

In the original variable, we have

r^{2j}\Phi^{0}_{j}(r^{2})=f_{j}(r)=\tilde{f}_{j}(r^{2})=\tilde{f}_{j}(u),

and thus

|\Phi^{0}_{j}(u)|\leq C

as desired.
∎

Next we estimate the Weyl-Titchmarsh function
\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)
with

-\mathcal{L}_{0}\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)=\xi\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi),\quad\xi>0,

namely

{\partial}_{rr}\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)+\frac{\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)}{4r^{2}}+\xi\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)=-\frac{8\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}.

We write

\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)=g(r\xi^{1/2}).

Then one has

g^{\prime\prime}(r\xi^{1/2})+\frac{1}{4r^{2}\xi}g(r\xi^{1/2})+g(r\xi^{1/2})=-\frac{8}{\xi(1+r^{2})^{2}}g(r\xi^{1/2}),

and

g^{\prime\prime}(q)+\frac{1}{4q^{2}}g(q)+g(q)=-\frac{8}{\xi(1+q^{2}/\xi)^{2}}g(q)

for

q:=r\xi^{1/2}.

Homogeneous solutions can be written in terms of special functions. Here we follow the argument in
[
34
, Proposition 5.6]
and
[
36
, Section 4]
. We now consider the case
r\xi^{1/2}\gtrsim 1
.

Lemma B.4
.

A Weyl-Titchmarsh function is of the form

\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)=\xi^{-1/4}e^{ir\xi^{1/2}}\sigma(r\xi^{1/2},r)\mbox{ \ for \ }r\xi^{1/2}\gtrsim 1

where
\sigma
admits the asymptotic series approximation about
q
with a fixed
r
,

\sigma(q,r)\sim\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\infty}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r),\quad\psi_{0}^{+}(r)=1,

\psi_{1}^{+}(r)=-\frac{i}{8}+i\left(\frac{2r^{2}}{r^{2}+1}+2r\arctan r-r\pi\right)

with

\sup_{r>0}|(r{\partial}_{r})^{k}\psi_{j}^{+}|\leq d_{jk},

and

|(r{\partial}_{r})^{\alpha}(q{\partial}_{q})^{\beta}(\sigma(q,r)-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r))|\leq e_{\alpha,\beta,j_{0}}q^{-j_{0}-1}.

Proof.

From the form of
\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)
, we only need to consider

\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sigma(r\xi^{1/2},r)=0.

We look for a formal power series that solves above equation

\sigma(r\xi^{1/2},r)=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\xi^{-j/2}f_{j}(r)

which implies the following recurrence relation

2i{\partial}_{r}f_{j}(r)=\left(-{\partial}_{rr}-\frac{1}{4r^{2}}-\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)f_{j-1}(r),\quad j\geq 1,~f_{0}(r)=1.

A solution is given by

f_{j}(r)=\frac{i}{2}{\partial}_{r}f_{j-1}(r)+\frac{i}{2}\int_{r}^{\infty}\left(-\frac{1}{4s^{2}}-\frac{8}{(1+s^{2})^{2}}\right)f_{j-1}(s)ds.

Then we get

|f_{1}(r)|\leq c_{10}r^{-1},\quad|(r{\partial}_{r})^{k}f_{1}|\leq c_{1k}r^{-1},~k\in\mathbb{N},

and by induction

|(r{\partial}_{r})^{k}f_{j}|\leq c_{jk}r^{-j},\quad j\geq 1,~r>0.

Now write

\psi_{j}^{+}(r):=r^{j}f_{j}(r),

and it follows from the control of
f_{j}
that

\sup_{r>0}|(r{\partial}_{r})^{k}\psi_{j}^{+}|\leq d_{jk}

for some constant
d_{jk}
. Write
q=r\xi^{1/2}
. Then

q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)=\xi^{-j/2}f_{j}(r).

Define

\sigma_{ap}(q,r):=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\infty}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)(1-\eta(q\delta_{j})),

where
\delta_{j}\rightarrow 0^{+}
sufficiently fast to ensure the convergence of the summation. Indeed, one has

\displaystyle\bigg|\sigma_{ap}(q,r)-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum\limits_{j=j_{0}+1}^{\infty}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)(1-\eta(q\delta_{j}))-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)\eta(q\delta_{j})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle e_{j_{0}}q^{-j_{0}-1}.

In the last step, we have used the following estimates. For
j\geq j_{0}+2
,

\big|q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)(1-\eta(q\delta_{j}))\big|\leq q^{-j}\mathbf{1}_{\{q\delta_{j}>1\}}d_{j0}\leq q^{-j_{0}-1}2^{-j}~\mbox{ if }~\delta_{j}d_{j0}\leq 2^{-j}.

For
0\leq j\leq j_{0}
,

|q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}\eta(q\delta_{j})|=|q^{-j_{0}-1}q^{j_{0}+1-j}\psi_{j}^{+}\eta(q\delta_{j})|\leq q^{-j_{0}-1}(2\delta_{j}^{-1})^{j_{0}+1}d_{j0}\leq q^{-j_{0}-1}\mathop{\max}\limits_{0\leq j\leq j_{0}}(2\delta_{j}^{-1})^{j_{0}+1}d_{j0}.

Similarly, we have

\bigg|(r{\partial}_{r})^{\alpha}(q{\partial}_{q})^{\beta}(\sigma_{ap}(q,r)-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r))\bigg|\leq e_{\alpha,\beta,j_{0}}q^{-j_{0}-1}.

This implies that
\sigma_{ap}(r\xi^{1/2},r)
is a good approximation at infinity, namely the error

\displaystyle e(r\xi^{1/2},r)=

\displaystyle\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sigma_{ap}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\left(\sigma_{ap}-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)+\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)\right)

is small. Indeed, for the first term, we estimate

\left|\left({\partial}_{rr}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\left(\sigma_{ap}-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)\right)\right|\lesssim r^{-2}q^{-j_{0}},

and

\left|2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}\left(\sigma_{ap}-\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)\right)\right|\lesssim\xi^{1/2}r^{-1}q^{-j_{0}-1}=r^{-2}q^{-j_{0}}.

For the second term, we have

\displaystyle\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}\xi^{-j/2}f_{j}(r)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left({\partial}_{rr}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}\xi^{-j/2}f_{j}(r)+2i\left(\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}-1}\xi^{-j/2}{\partial}_{r}f_{j+1}(r)\right)

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle\left({\partial}_{rr}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\xi^{-{j_{0}}/2}f_{j_{0}}(r),

so

\left|\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sum\limits_{j=0}^{j_{0}}q^{-j}\psi_{j}^{+}(r)\right|\lesssim\xi^{-{j_{0}}/2}r^{-j_{0}-2}=r^{-2}q^{-j_{0}}.

Therefore, the error has the following bound

\big|e(r\xi^{1/2},r)\big|\leq c_{j_{0}}r^{-2}q^{-j_{0}}\mbox{ \ for all \ }j_{0}\geq 0.

We then look for perturbation
\sigma_{1}=-\sigma+\sigma_{ap}
solved by

\left({\partial}_{rr}+2i\xi^{1/2}{\partial}_{r}+\frac{1}{4r^{2}}+\frac{8}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)\sigma_{1}=e(r\xi^{1/2},r),

which can be formulated, by writing
(v_{1},v_{2})=(\sigma,r{\partial}_{r}\sigma)
, as a system of first order

(B.2)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &{\partial}_{r}v_{1}=r^{-1}v_{2}\\
&{\partial}_{r}v_{2}=\left(-\frac{1}{4r}-\frac{8r}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right)v_{1}+(r^{-1}-2i\xi^{1/2})v_{2}+re(r\xi^{1/2},r)\\
\end{aligned}\right.

We then estimate

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\partial}_{r}(|v_{1}|^{2}+|v_{2}|^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\rm Re}(\bar{v}_{1}{\partial}_{r}v_{1})+2{\rm Re}(\bar{v}_{2}{\partial}_{r}v_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2r^{-1}{\rm Re}(\bar{v}_{1}v_{2})+2{\rm Re}\Big[\bar{v}_{2}\Big(\Big(-\frac{1}{4r}-\frac{8r}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\Big)v_{1}+(r^{-1}-2i\xi^{1/2})v_{2}+re(r\xi^{1/2},r)\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2r^{-1}{\rm Re}(\bar{v}_{1}v_{2})-\left(\frac{1}{2r}+\frac{16r}{(1+r^{2})^{2}}\right){\rm Re}(\bar{v}_{2}v_{1})+2r^{-1}|v_{2}|^{2}+2r{\rm Re}\big(\bar{v}_{2}e(r\xi^{1/2},r)\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-C(r^{-1}|v|^{2}+r|v||e(r\xi^{1/2},r)|)

for some
C>0
. So we have

{\partial}_{r}|v|\geq-C(r^{-1}|v|+r|e(r\xi^{1/2},r)|),

and

|v|\leq Cr^{-C}\int_{r}^{\infty}s|e(s\xi^{1/2},s)|ds.

Recalling the estimate

|e|\lesssim r^{-2}q^{-j_{0}}=r^{-2-j_{0}}\xi^{-j_{0}/2},

we get

|v|\lesssim r^{-j_{0}}\xi^{-j_{0}/2}.

For the estimate of higher order derivatives
(r{\partial}_{r})^{\alpha}(\xi{\partial}_{\xi})^{\beta}v
, one needs to differentiate (
B.2
) and repeat above process. The argument is a verbatim repetition of the proof in
[
34
]
.
∎

By the asymptotic expansion of
\Psi_{0}^{+}
, we have
W(\Psi_{0}^{+},\overline{\Psi_{0}^{+}})=-2i
. Concerning the density of spectral measure, we have the following

Proposition B.5
.

We have

(B.3)

\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)=a_{0}(\xi)\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)+\overline{a_{0}(\xi)\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)},

where

|a_{0}(\xi)|\sim 1.

The spectral measure
\rho_{0}
has density estimate

\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)\sim|a_{0}(\xi)|^{-2}\sim 1.

Proof.

We follow the argument in
[
34
, Proposition 5.7]
.

a_{0}(\xi)=\frac{i}{2}W(\Phi^{0},\overline{\Psi_{0}^{+}})=\frac{i}{2}(\Phi^{0}(r,\xi){\partial}_{r}\overline{\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)}-\overline{\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)}{\partial}_{r}\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)).

We evaluate the Wronskian in the region where both the
\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)
and
\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)
asymptotics are useful, i.e., where
r^{2}\xi\sim 1
. Recall that for
r^{2}\xi\sim 1
, one has

\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)\sim\frac{r^{1/2}(r^{2}-1)}{r^{2}+1}+r^{1/2}\Phi_{1}^{0}(r^{2}),

and

\Phi_{1}^{0}(u)\sim\left\{\begin{aligned} &-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{u}{4}+O(u^{2})~&\mbox{ for }~0<u\leq 1,\\
&\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\log^{2}u}{2u}+O\left(\frac{\log u}{u}\right)~&\mbox{ for }~u\geq 1.\\
\end{aligned}\right.

For all
\xi>0
, it follows from Lemma
B.4
that

|\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)|\lesssim\xi^{-1/4},\quad|{\partial}_{r}\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)|\lesssim\xi^{1/4}.

For
r\sim\xi^{-1/2}
, we have

|\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)|\lesssim\xi^{-1/4},\quad|{\partial}_{r}\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)|\lesssim\xi^{1/4},

and thus

|a_{0}(\xi)|\lesssim 1.

Therefore, we have

\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)\sim|a_{0}(\xi)|^{-2}\gtrsim 1.

In order to get the lower bound of
|a_{0}(\xi)|
, by (
B.3
), we have

|a_{0}(\xi)|\geq\frac{|\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)|}{2|\Psi_{0}^{+}(r,\xi)|}.

For
0<\xi\leq 1
, we take
r=M\xi^{-1/2}
with a sufficiently large
M
. Then above estimates imply

|\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)|\gtrsim\xi^{-1/4}.

For
\xi\geq 1,
we similarly take
r=m\xi^{-1/2}
for a sufficiently small
m
. Then

|\Phi^{0}(r,\xi)|\gtrsim\xi^{-1/4}.

Collecting above estimates, we conclude the estimate of density

\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)\sim 1

as desired.
∎

B.2.
Duhamel’s representation by DFT

Now we formulate the Duhamel’s formula for the linearization at mode 0

\partial_{\tau}\phi=\partial_{\rho\rho}\phi+\frac{1}{\rho}\partial_{\rho}\phi+V_{0}\phi+h,

where

V_{0}=\frac{8}{(1+\rho^{2})^{2}},

and
h
has fast decay in space-time. If we take
\phi=\rho^{-1/2}A
, we get

\partial_{\tau}A=A^{\prime\prime}+\frac{A}{4\rho^{2}}+V_{0}A+h\rho^{1/2}=\mathcal{L}_{0}A+h\rho^{1/2}.

Consider the Cauchy problem

(B.4)

\begin{cases}\partial_{\tau}A=\mathcal{L}_{0}A+h\rho^{1/2},\\
A(\cdot,t_{0})=0.\\
\end{cases}

For

\mathcal{L}_{0}\Phi^{0}=-\xi\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi),

the generalized eigenfunction satisfies the following pointwise upper bound

(B.5)

|\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)|\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &\rho^{1/2},\quad&\rho^{2}\xi\ll 1,\\
&\xi^{-1/4},\quad&\rho^{2}\xi\gg 1,\\
\end{aligned}\right.

and its associated spectral measure has density estimate

(B.6)

\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)\sim 1.

Taking distorted Fourier transform on both sides of (
B.4
), we obtain

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{\tau}\hat{A}(\xi,{\tau})=-\xi\hat{A}(\xi,{\tau})+\int_{0}^{\infty}h\rho^{1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)d\rho,\\
&\hat{A}(\cdot,{\tau}_{0})=0.\\
\end{aligned}\right.

Therefore, one has

\phi(\rho,{\tau})=\rho^{-1/2}A(\rho,{\tau})=\int_{{\tau}_{0}}^{\tau}\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)\Phi^{0}(x,\xi)x^{1/2}h(x,s)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)d\xi dxds.

Now assume the RHS
h
is smooth enough with the pointwise space-time control

|h(\rho,{\tau})|\lesssim v(\tau)\langle\rho\rangle^{-\ell}\|h\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell},\quad~\ell>\frac{3}{2},

where
v(\tau)
is a regular function decay in
\tau
. Then

|\phi|\lesssim\|h\|^{(\tau)}_{v,\ell}\int_{{\tau}_{0}}^{\tau}ds\int_{0}^{\infty}v(s)e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)d\xi\int_{0}^{\infty}\Phi^{0}(x,\xi)x^{1/2}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx.

Before estimating
|\phi|
, we first invoke a lemma that will frequently used below. The following lemma is proved in
[
55
, Lemma A.3]
.

Lemma B.6

(
[
55
]
)
.

Assume constants
a
,
b
satisfy either
a>-1
or
a=-1
and
b<-1
. For
0\leq x_{0}\leq x_{1}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we have

(B.7)

\displaystyle\int_{x_{0}}^{x_{1}}e^{-\lambda x}x^{a}(-\log x)^{b}dx\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}x_{1}^{a+1}(-\log x_{1})^{b}&\mbox{ \ if \ }a>-1\\
(-\log x_{1})^{b+1}-(-\log x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }a=-1,b<-1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }0\leq\lambda\leq x_{1}^{-1}\\
\frac{(\log\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}+\begin{cases}0&\mbox{ \ if \ }a>-1\\
(\log\lambda)^{b+1}-(-\log x_{0})^{b+1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }a=-1,b<-1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }x_{1}^{-1}\leq\lambda\leq x_{0}^{-1}\\
\frac{(\log\lambda)^{b}}{\lambda^{a+1}}e^{-\frac{x_{0}\lambda}{2}}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\lambda\geq x_{0}^{-1}\end{cases}.

The linear theory for the mode
0
is stated as follows, and its proof is in a similar spirit as
[
55
, Proposition 9.8]
.

Proof of Proposition
7.4
.

We first estimate the Duhamel’s representation without imposing orthogonality on the RHS
h(\rho,\tau)
and compute

\displaystyle|F(\xi)|:=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\Phi^{0}(x,\xi)x^{1/2}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx\right|\lesssim\int_{0}^{\xi^{-1/2}}x\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx+\xi^{-1/4}\int_{\xi^{-1/2}}^{\infty}x^{1/2}\langle x\rangle^{-\ell}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\xi^{-1/4}~&\mbox{ for }~\xi\geq 1,\\
\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1},~&\ell<2\\
\log\xi,~&\ell=2\\
1,~&\ell>2\\
\end{cases}~&\mbox{ for }~\xi\leq 1.\\
\end{cases}

Now we consider the integration in
\xi
:

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)F(\xi)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)d\xi\right|=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{\rho^{-2}}+\int_{\rho^{-2}}^{\infty}\right)\cdots:=P_{1}+P_{2}.

\bullet
For
P_{1}
, if
\rho>1
, then we have

\displaystyle|P_{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\rho^{-2}}e^{-\xi(\tau-s)}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\log\xi&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~d\xi\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\rho^{-\ell}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-2}\langle\log\rho\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-2}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

If
\rho\leq 1
, then one has

\displaystyle P_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\rho^{-2}}e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)F(\xi)d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{0}^{1}+\int_{1}^{\rho^{-2}}\right)e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)F(\xi)d\xi

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle P_{11}+P_{12},

where we estimate

\displaystyle|P_{11}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1}e^{-\xi(\tau-s)}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\log\xi&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~d\xi\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle|P_{22}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{1}^{\rho^{-2}}e^{-\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-1/4}d\xi\lesssim\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}.

Hence one obtains for
\rho\leq 1

|P_{1}|\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-\frac{3}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

\bullet
For
P_{2}
, if
\rho\leq 1
, we estimate

\displaystyle|P_{2}|\lesssim\rho^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\int_{\rho^{-2}}^{\infty}e^{-\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}d\xi\lesssim\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}.

If
\rho>1
, then we have

\displaystyle P_{2}=

\displaystyle\int^{\infty}_{\rho^{-2}}e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)F(\xi)d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\rho^{-2}}^{1}+\int_{1}^{\infty}\right)e^{-\xi({\tau}-s)}\rho^{-1/2}\Phi^{0}(\rho,\xi)\frac{d\rho_{0}}{d\xi}(\xi)F(\xi)d\xi

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle P_{21}+P_{22},

where

\displaystyle|P_{21}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\rho^{-2}}^{1}e^{-\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-1/4}\begin{cases}\xi^{\frac{\ell}{2}-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\log\xi&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho^{-1/2}\begin{cases}\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{2\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

and

\displaystyle|P_{22}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\rho^{-1/2}\int_{1}^{\infty}e^{-\xi(\tau-s)}\xi^{-\frac{1}{2}}d\xi\lesssim\rho^{-1/2}\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}

Therefore, we obtain for
\rho>1
that

|P_{2}|\lesssim\rho^{-1/2}\begin{cases}\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{4\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{4\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}e^{-\frac{\tau-s}{4\rho^{2}}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

Collecting above estimates, we conclude that

\bullet
for
\rho\leq 1
,

|P_{1}+P_{2}|\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\rho^{2}<\tau-s\leq 1\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>1\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2\end{cases},

\bullet
for
\rho>1
,

|P_{1}+P_{2}|\lesssim\begin{cases}\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{l}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell<2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell=2\\
\begin{cases}\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s\leq 1\\
\rho^{-1/2}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }1<\tau-s\leq\rho^{2}\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\tau-s>\rho^{2}\end{cases}&\mbox{ \ for \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

We now estimate the convolution in time.

\bullet
For
\rho\leq 1
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\phi(\rho,\tau)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle~\left(\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}\right)v(s)\Big|P_{1}(\rho,\tau,s)+P_{2}(\rho,\tau,s)\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}ds+v(\tau)\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}ds

\displaystyle~+\begin{cases}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)(\tau-s)^{-1}ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~v(\tau)+\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)(\log\tau)^{2}+\tau^{-1}\log\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\log\tau+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

\bullet
For
1<\rho\leq(\frac{\tau}{2})^{1/2}
, we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\phi(\rho,\tau)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle~\left(\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}\right)v(s)\Big|P_{1}(\rho,\tau,s)+P_{2}(\rho,\tau,s)\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~v(\tau)\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}ds+v(\tau)\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~ds

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}v(s)\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)+v(\tau)\begin{cases}\rho^{2-\ell}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\log\rho\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
1&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}+\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)\int_{\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\langle\log z\rangle z^{-1}dz+\tau^{-1}\log\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\log(\frac{\tau}{2\rho^{2}})+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)(\langle\log\rho\rangle+\int_{\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}\langle\log z\rangle z^{-1}dz)+\tau^{-1}\log\tau\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\langle\log(\frac{\tau}{2\rho^{2}})\rangle+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

\bullet
For
(\frac{\tau}{2})^{1/2}\leq\rho\leq\tau^{1/2}
, we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\phi(\rho,\tau)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle~\left(\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}+\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}\right)v(s)\Big|P_{1}(\rho,\tau,s)+P_{2}(\rho,\tau,s)\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}ds+\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~ds

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}v(s)\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
(\tau-s)^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)+\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}+\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\tau-\rho^{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-\rho^{2}}v(s)ds\begin{cases}\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{-1}\langle\log\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{-1}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}+\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds\begin{cases}\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle~\begin{cases}\tau^{1-\frac{\ell}{2}}v(\tau)+\tau^{-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
\langle\log\tau\rangle v(\tau)+\tau^{-1}\langle\log\tau\rangle\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)+\tau^{-1}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

\bullet
For
\rho\geq\tau^{1/2}
, we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\phi(\rho,\tau)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle~\left(\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}+\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}\right)v(s)\Big|P_{1}(\rho,\tau,s)+P_{2}(\rho,\tau,s)\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)\int_{\tau-1}^{\tau}(\tau-s)^{-\frac{1}{2}}ds+\rho^{-1/2}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\tau-1}v(s)\begin{cases}(\tau-s)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log(\tau-s)\rangle&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3}{4}}&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}~~ds

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}v(\tau)+\rho^{-1/2}\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\rho^{-1/2}\begin{cases}v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{5}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{\ell}{2}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell<2\\
v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\langle\log\tau\rangle\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell=2\\
v(\tau)\tau^{\frac{1}{4}}+\tau^{-\frac{3}{4}}\int_{\frac{\tau_{0}}{2}}^{\frac{\tau}{2}}v(s)ds&\mbox{ \ if \ }\ell>2\end{cases}.

Collecting above estimates, we obtain the a priori estimates without orthogonality. Estimates with orthogonality can be derived in a similar manner, where the orthogonality condition

\int_{0}^{\infty}h(\rho,\tau)\frac{\rho^{2}-1}{\rho^{2}+1}\rho d\rho=0\quad\forall\tau\in(\tau_{0},+\infty)

is used when estimating

\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\Phi^{0}(x,\xi)x^{1/2}h(x,s)dx\right|=\Bigg|\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\Phi^{0}(x,\xi)-\frac{x^{1/2}(x^{2}-1)}{x^{2}+1}\right)x^{1/2}h(x,s)dx\Bigg|.

We refer the interested readers to
[
55
, Proposition 9.8]
.
∎
